#+TITLE: Claude Code idle-compaction PTY supervisor
#+DESCRIPTION: Canonical source for the PTY-owning ordinary Claude launcher
#+AUTHOR: Mètsàtron
#+PROPERTY: header-args :mkdirp yes :comments both

* Design notes

This launcher is the owner for ordinary interactive Claude Code processes. It
uses Python's =pty.fork= so the Claude child receives a real controlling
terminal while the parent owns the PTY master for the entire process lifetime.
The parent proxies bytes in both directions, propagates terminal geometry and
signals, and is the only component permitted to write the automatic =/compact=
submission.  Claude hook helpers communicate with the per-session Unix socket;
they never write to a terminal.

The fallback values intentionally match the Emacs governor: 55 minutes and a
60-minute warm-cache TTL for the long profile, 4 minutes and 5 minutes for the
short profile, and an absolute 70,000 current input-context threshold.  When
the fleet cache policy has fresh supported evidence for the current endpoint
and status-line model, the supervisor resolves it once while arming and again
immediately before automatic action.  A later action deadline re-arms instead
of compacting early.  Missing, stale, observed-only, or malformed policy falls
back to the profile values; the environment variables documented in =--help=
remain the explicit fallback surface.

Re-arm law (sealed 2026-08-21, the second overnight no-compact hunt).  The
timer is not one-shot-until-next-Stop.  It fires =IDLE_SECONDS= after the most
recent activity of any kind (Stop, local keystroke, lifecycle hook event,
user-attributed transcript record), and every cancellation or gate refusal
that is not structurally dormant re-arms it (=rearmed= in the guard log, with
the reason).  Dormant reasons are the ones only a new Stop, a control command,
or a larger context can clear: disabled, clean, below the token threshold,
unbound, not running, already compacting.  Everything else, including a
failed PTY-ownership probe, retries after =CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_RETRY_SECONDS=.
A cold cache does not veto; the =compaction-requested= event records warm or
cold.  A live turn does not veto once the idle window has passed: the
injected =/compact= queues behind the running turn and never kills a
background process; if Claude answers after compaction and then goes quiet,
the governor compacts again an idle window later.  A compaction that produces
neither =PostCompact= nor Claude's rejection marker within
=CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS= is declared failed and re-armed.  The
PTY-ownership probe is retried three times and logs which condition failed
(=pty-ownership-check-failed=), because the 2026-08-20 Fable session lost its
only armed compaction to a single unexplained probe failure at 16:08 and then
sat dormant for twenty hours, while the Opus session lost its own to one
keystroke at 22:33 with the same dormancy.

Last-session record (sealed 2026-08-21, the Claudeificus revive): the runtime dir under `$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/claude-idle-compaction/<channel>/` is removed on exit, which left a bot that had just quit with no recoverable session id — and the operator sad. The supervisor now writes `~/.local/state/claude-idle-compaction/<channel>.last-session.json` and, when `CLAUDE_WARM_HERDR_AGENT` names the bot, `last-session.<Label>.json`, on every session bind and again at exit. `pokedex nurse` and `nurse-joy revive` read the label file as the resume source for a down bot; it is the record of the last truth, never a guess.


Resolver output is untrusted: only exact endpoint/model identity, supported
confidence and positive ordered integer windows select measured timing.
Malformed output retains this consumer's fixed fallback. Automatic actions
refresh policy and current declared model at their final gate; a later deadline
preserves the idle window. Custom endpoint identity is never inferred.
A rejected current snapshot cannot reuse the previous model for measured timing;
its window falls back while the existing owner/session gate remains authoritative.

* Versioned integration notes

The Claude-side adapter was checked against the installed Claude Code
=2.1.220= integration.  =Stop= uses the documented =stop_hook_active= field
and accepts only the literal false value; =last_assistant_message= is not
forwarded because it is not needed by the governor.  =UserPromptSubmit= and
=PostCompact= are routed by their documented event names; the adapter forwards
only =session_id=, =transcript_path= on =SessionStart=, and the fixed event
symbol.  The adapter deliberately does not read or invent =background_tasks=
or =session_crons= fields.

Claude Code =2.1.23x= adds transcript bookkeeping records the classifier must
know: =atis-latch= (empty latch marker flushed right after Stop),
=file-history-delta= (per-edit backup tracking beside the existing
=file-history-snapshot=), =bridge-session= (remote-bridge session metadata),
and the =system/compact_boundary= subtype.  Left unknown they set the
fail-closed =transcript-uncertain= gate after every turn and veto each armed
idle compaction at fire time.  The same 2026-08-20 hunt sealed the
foreign-session guard: hook-spawned nested one-shot claudes (the PromptGolf
swing classifier, bridge calls) inherit this supervisor's control-socket
environment, and their own =SessionStart=/=SessionEnd= would rebind the
governor to the one-shot and mark it ended, locking the real session out as
=session-mismatch= for the rest of its life.  The supervisor now pins the
project directory of the first bound transcript and ignores =session-start=
events whose transcript lives elsewhere (=foreign-session-ignored= in the
guard log); nested spawners additionally scrub =CLAUDE_IDLE_*= from the
environment before invoking =claude -p=.

The installed status-line schema supplies =session_id= and
=context_window.total_input_tokens=, with the documented
=current_usage.input_tokens=, =cache_creation_input_tokens=, and
=cache_read_input_tokens= sum as fallback.  Output tokens are excluded.  The
snapshot is the only token source for this supervisor; transcript content is
watched only for post-Stop activity.

The owner contract is explicit: =CLAUDE_IDLE_OWNER=emacs= belongs to
=claude-code-ide.el=, =CLAUDE_IDLE_OWNER=pty-supervisor= belongs to this
launcher, and sessions launched by neither owner remain unsupported legacy
processes.  This source does not replace the =claude= executable globally.

Herdr 0.7.4 / protocol 16 evidence (verified on ThinkPad-T480s 2026-08-04):
the supported pane contract is =pane.report_agent= (pane_id, source, agent,
state, optional agent_session_id), =pane.report_agent_session=,
=pane.report_metadata= (display_agent, tokens, ttl_ms), and
=pane.release_agent=.  The =agent= field accepts the logical labels Opus,
Fable, and Auryn.  Herdr's installed Claude hook hard-codes =claude= and was
not present in the active settings, so this PTY owner reports directly through
the inherited =HERDR_SOCKET_PATH= after spawning Claude.  The reporter uses
=source= =claude-warm=, the inherited =HERDR_PANE_ID=, the real Claude child
PID in metadata, and a bounded heartbeat/TTL; no PTY bytes or prompt data pass
through this API.

Herdr's native detector does not see through the outer =claude-warm= process on
the installed Claude path, so the supervisor mirrors only visible state from
the PTY output it already proxies.  The screen adapter recognizes Claude's
prompt, working, and confirmation/cancellation markers conservatively; it
never rewrites or suppresses output.  Lifecycle hooks remain responsible only
for the private compaction state machine and session identity refresh.

The supervisor's stdin path forwards every byte unchanged but uses a stateful
escape parser for governor activity.  SGR/legacy mouse, focus, cursor-position,
device-status/device-attribute, OSC/DCS negotiation, and bracketed-paste
framing are terminal protocol; printable/editing bytes, paste payloads, SS3,
CSI-u, and Kitty keyboard input are human activity.  An incomplete escape is
held across reads for a bounded interval before becoming privacy-safe unknown
input.  Protocol traffic is never treated as prompt editing, and one armed
timer can emit only one =cancelled:user-input= transition.  Supervisor debug
output is written to the session debug file, never to the foreground terminal.
At teardown, after Claude has exited, the owner restores the outer termios and
resets the full-screen mouse, focus, alternate-screen, bracketed-paste, cursor,
and SGR modes it may have exposed through the proxy.

Claude Code =2.1.220= appends =system= entries with subtypes
=stop_hook_summary= and =turn_duration= immediately around a normal Stop
notification, and also emits =last-prompt=, =mode=, =permission-mode=,
=ai-title=, and (when the session is away) =away_summary= metadata entries.
The observed delayed production record was a =system/away_summary= entry, with
no message role, and was recap bookkeeping rather than a new turn.  The
transcript guard therefore classifies complete JSONL records: only a real
non-meta =user= record cancels an armed timer; known assistant, recap,
progress, queue, file-history, channel bookkeeping, and completion records are
ignored without moving the deadline.  Malformed or unknown records are
recorded as uncertainty and block the final injection gate without cancelling
the timer.  A later local input, UserPromptSubmit, verified inbound channel
turn, or documented lifecycle event remains authoritative activity.

When Claude rejects an injected =/compact= because the session is too small,
the installed version records adjacent user entries for the slash command and
a =system/local_command= JSONL entry containing the fixed =Not enough
messages= marker, without emitting =PostCompact=.  The supervisor treats that
source-backed shape as an asynchronous compaction failure: it clears
=compacting=, retains =dirty=, and ignores the command's possible later Stop so
the failed request cannot start an idle-compaction loop.  Other transcript
activity is ignored or gate-blocked according to its classification; only a
genuinely user-attributed record remains a transcript cancellation signal.

The channel guard is deliberately generic.  It derives watched server names
from =--channels= (or the explicit
=CLAUDE_IDLE_WATCHED_SERVERS= override), and never embeds a Telegram bot name,
chat identifier, or message.  Claude Code 2.1.220 and the installed Telegram
plugin 0.0.6 emit attachment deltas whose observed server identity is
=plugin:telegram:telegram=; that identity is therefore the derived default
for that channel specification.  The slash-command response itself remains
overrideable with =CLAUDE_IDLE_RECONNECT_SERVER= until a healthy live session
has confirmed the exact =/mcp reconnect= command path.

Channel recovery uses the same final PTY gate as compaction.  It watches only
new transcript JSONL lines, treats watched =removedNames= entries as channel
death, waits for a matching watched =addedNames= entry, and gives up after two
reconnect attempts plus one =/reload-plugins= attempt.  The persistent guard
log is private state under =XDG_STATE_HOME=; it contains event names and safe
diagnostics, never prompts or channel message contents.

Claude Code =2.1.291='s development-channel confirmation was verified in the
installed =~/.npm-global/lib/node_modules/@anthropic-ai/claude-code/bin/claude.exe=
bundle.  Its embedded =chunk-npatgfzx.js= names the title, both warning lines,
the exact =Channels:= entries, the affirmative label, and the exit label.  The
startup accepter requires that complete source-backed signature after ANSI
removal, requires every displayed entry to be explicitly opted in, and sends
one Enter at most.  A changed prompt, an unlisted entry, local input, any first
turn lifecycle event, or expiry of the bounded startup window closes it without
writing to the PTY.

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/bin/claude-warm :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env python3" :comments no
"""PTY-owning Claude launcher and per-session idle-compaction supervisor."""

from __future__ import annotations

import codecs
import ctypes
import datetime
import errno
import hashlib
import fcntl
import json
import math
import os
import pathlib
import pty
import queue
import re
import selectors
import shutil
import signal
import socket
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import termios
import threading
import time
import tty
import uuid
import unicodedata


CHANNEL_RE = re.compile(r"^claude-[0-9a-f]{32}$")
SESSION_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,240}$")
EVENTS = {
    "session-start",
    "stop",
    "user-prompt-submit",
    "post-compact",
    "session-activity",
    "stop-failure",
    "session-end",
}
CONTROL_COMMANDS = {
    "state",
    "show",
    "enable",
    "disable",
    "profile",
    "cancel",
    "trigger",
    "inject",
    "preserve",
}

INJECT_RE = re.compile(r"^/mcp reconnect ([A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{1,128})$")
CHANNEL_DEATH_MARKER = "removedNames"
CHANNEL_ATTACH_MARKER = "addedNames"
DEVELOPMENT_CHANNEL_ENTRY_PATTERN = (
    r"(?:server:[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{1,160}|"
    r"plugin:[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{1,96}@[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{1,96})"
)
DEVELOPMENT_CHANNEL_ENTRY_RE = re.compile(
    rf"^{DEVELOPMENT_CHANNEL_ENTRY_PATTERN}$"
)
EXPECTED_COMPLETION_SYSTEM_SUBTYPES = frozenset(
    {"stop_hook_summary", "turn_duration", "away_summary"}
)
EXPECTED_COMPLETION_TRANSCRIPT_TYPES = frozenset(
    {"last-prompt", "mode", "permission-mode", "ai-title", "atis-latch"}
)
TRANSCRIPT_INTERNAL_SYSTEM_SUBTYPES = frozenset(
    {
        "stop_hook_summary",
        "turn_duration",
        "away_summary",
        "local_command",
        "hook_progress",
        "hook_result",
        "progress",
        "status",
        "queue",
        "queue-operation",
        "file-history-snapshot",
        "summary",
        "recap",
        "channel_send_failure",
        "channel_send_retry",
        "channel_send_error",
        # Compaction writes its boundary marker as a system record; it is
        # governor-internal bookkeeping, never a human or channel turn.
        "compact_boundary",
        # A notice-level meta line ("Compacted while idle, before the prompt
        # cache expired"), written after idle compaction; never a turn.
        "informational",
    }
)
TRANSCRIPT_INTERNAL_TYPES = frozenset(
    {
        "last-prompt",
        "mode",
        "permission-mode",
        "ai-title",
        "progress",
        "status",
        "queue",
        "queue-operation",
        "file-history-snapshot",
        # Claude 2.1.23x completion bookkeeping: atis-latch lands right after
        # Stop on Fable/Opus turns and file-history-delta accompanies edits.
        # Left unknown they set the transcript-uncertain gate after every
        # turn, which vetoes each armed idle compaction at fire time (the
        # 2026-08-20 overnight no-compact hunt).
        "atis-latch",
        "file-history-delta",
        # frame-link joined the same bookkeeping family in 2.1.236 (first seen
        # 2026-08-29 on Fable's channel: 216 unknown-record latches, 555
        # gate-refused cycles, two no-compact nights before diagnosis).
        "frame-link",
        # Claude's own remote-bridge metadata record (bridgeSessionId/owner
        # UUIDs) — session plumbing, not conversation content.
        "bridge-session",
        "summary",
        "recap",
        # Claude 2.1.31x request and session bookkeeping, seen 2026-11-06 on
        # every warm seat. Each carries a sessionId and plumbing only: request
        # digests and shapes (api-request, api-request-blob,
        # api-request-shape), the session's agent name, the dev-mods folder,
        # running cost totals and the tools-used list. Left unknown they
        # latched transcript_gate_blocked after every turn — 14,695 refusals
        # across the seats — so no MailCortex wake was admitted and idle
        # compaction was vetoed (Fable's wake lane, Admiral 10848).
        "api-request",
        "api-request-blob",
        "api-request-shape",
        "agent-name",
        "dev-mods",
        "cost-state",
        "tools-used",
        # session-owner carries only account and organisation UUIDs for the
        # session; it appeared on the first turn after the 2026-11-06 revive
        # and latched the gate again on its own.
        "session-owner",
    }
)
COMPACTION_FAILURE_MARKER = "not enough messages"
# Claude prints "Not compacted · <reason>" when a session.compact hook skips
# the compaction; the decider-summary mod does this whenever the local model
# cannot produce a summary.  No PostCompact and no Stop follow, so a governor
# that misses this line holds the seat `active' for COMPACT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
# and every MailCortex wake in that window is refused as busy (2026-11-18,
# Fable: three failed decider compactions from 14:09 to 15:04, five mails
# refused until their wake attempts ran out).
COMPACTION_SKIP_MARKER = "not compacted"
# Consecutive failed decider compactions back off: 30 min, 1 h, 2 h ... 6 h.
DECIDER_COMPACT_BACKOFF_SECONDS = 30 * 60
DECIDER_COMPACT_BACKOFF_MAX_SECONDS = 6 * 60 * 60
# A rejection line is appended after the injection; its embedded timestamp
# can trail the governor's own clock by the PTY write, so allow this slack.
COMPACTION_MARKER_SLACK_SECONDS = 5.0
USAGE_LIMIT_RE = re.compile(
    r"hit your [\w -]*limit\b.*?resets\s+(?:([A-Za-z]{3,9})\s+(\d{1,2}),\s*)?"
    r"(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2}))?\s*(am|pm)\s*(?:\(([\w/+-]+)\))?",
    re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL,
)
USAGE_LIMIT_UNPARSED_HOLD_SECONDS = 60 * 60
USAGE_LIMIT_MONTHS = ("jan", "feb", "mar", "apr", "may", "jun", "jul", "aug", "sep", "oct", "nov", "dec")


def is_usage_limit_text(text: object) -> bool:
    """True for Claude's fixed "You've hit your ... limit" refusal."""
    return isinstance(text, str) and bool(re.search(r"hit your [\w -]*limit\b", text, re.IGNORECASE))


def parse_usage_limit_reset(text: object, *, at: float | None = None) -> float | None:
    """The reset instant named by a usage-limit refusal, or None if unreadable.

    Forms seen: "resets 10:10pm (Australia/Melbourne)" and "resets Nov 5, 6pm (...)".
    A bare clock time means its next occurrence.
    """
    if not isinstance(text, str):
        return None
    match = USAGE_LIMIT_RE.search(text)
    if match is None:
        return None
    month, day, hour, minute, meridiem, zone_name = match.groups()
    try:
        from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
        zone = ZoneInfo(zone_name) if zone_name else None
    except Exception:
        zone = None
    reference = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(time.time() if at is None else at, zone)
    hour_24 = int(hour) % 12 + (12 if meridiem.lower() == "pm" else 0)
    try:
        if month:
            month_number = USAGE_LIMIT_MONTHS.index(month[:3].lower()) + 1
            reset = reference.replace(month=month_number, day=int(day), hour=hour_24,
                                      minute=int(minute or 0), second=0, microsecond=0)
            if (reference - reset).days > 180:
                reset = reset.replace(year=reset.year + 1)
        else:
            reset = reference.replace(hour=hour_24, minute=int(minute or 0), second=0, microsecond=0)
            if reset <= reference:
                reset += datetime.timedelta(days=1)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return reset.timestamp()


def env_int(name: str, default: int, minimum: int = 0) -> int:
    """Read a bounded integer configuration value from the environment."""
    try:
        return max(minimum, int(os.environ.get(name, str(default))))
    except ValueError:
        return default


def env_float(name: str, default: float, minimum: float = 0.0) -> float:
    """Read a bounded floating-point configuration value from the environment."""
    try:
        return max(minimum, float(os.environ.get(name, str(default))))
    except ValueError:
        return default


PRESERVATION_FEDERATION_SCHEMA = "fleet.preservation-sync-request.v1"
PRESERVATION_FEDERATION_RESPONSE_SCHEMA = "fleet.preservation-sync.v1"
PRESERVATION_BRIDGE_ADAPTER = "federated_preservation.SshAuthorityTransport"
PRESERVATION_BRIDGE_PROTOCOL = "stdin-json-v1"
PRESERVATION_HOST_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,62}$")
PRESERVATION_BINDING_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{1,160}$")
PRESERVATION_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{1,240}$")
PRESERVATION_MAX_BYTES = 64 * 1024
_preservation_poll_seconds = env_float(
    "CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_POLL_SECONDS", 30.0, 0.1
)
PRESERVATION_POLL_SECONDS = (
    _preservation_poll_seconds if math.isfinite(_preservation_poll_seconds) else 30.0
)
_preservation_bridge_timeout_seconds = env_float(
    "CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_BRIDGE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS", 8.0, 0.1
)
PRESERVATION_BRIDGE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = min(
    _preservation_bridge_timeout_seconds
    if math.isfinite(_preservation_bridge_timeout_seconds)
    else 8.0,
    8.0,
)
# A completed worker must become visible promptly, but an in-flight bridge
# must never turn the owner loop into a zero-timeout poller.  This cap is only
# a wake bound; it is not a second preservation deadline.
PRESERVATION_IN_FLIGHT_WAKE_SECONDS = 0.25


def channel_arguments(argv: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    """Return channel specifications without changing the child argv."""
    specifications: list[str] = []
    index = 0
    while index < len(argv):
        argument = argv[index]
        if argument.startswith("--channels="):
            specifications.extend(
                value for value in argument.split("=", 1)[1].split(",") if value
            )
        elif argument == "--channels":
            index += 1
            while index < len(argv) and not argv[index].startswith("-"):
                specifications.extend(value for value in argv[index].split(",") if value)
                index += 1
            continue
        index += 1
    return specifications


def accepted_development_channels() -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Return the exact, validated startup-confirmation opt-in entries."""
    configured = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_WARM_ACCEPT_DEV_CHANNELS", "")
    if not configured:
        return ()
    entries = [entry.strip() for entry in configured.split(",")]
    if not entries or any(
        not entry or DEVELOPMENT_CHANNEL_ENTRY_RE.fullmatch(entry) is None
        for entry in entries
    ):
        return ()
    return tuple(dict.fromkeys(entries))


def watched_server_names(argv: list[str]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Derive safe attachment names from --channels or an explicit override."""
    override = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_IDLE_WATCHED_SERVERS", "")
    if override:
        candidates = [value.strip() for value in override.split(",") if value.strip()]
    else:
        candidates = []
        for specification in channel_arguments(argv):
            base = specification.split("@", 1)[0]
            if not base:
                continue
            candidates.append(base)
            parts = [part for part in base.split(":") if part]
            if len(parts) >= 2:
                # Claude's plugin attachment names are derived from the
                # channel namespace and plugin name.  Keep both observed
                # spellings available without hard-coding a provider.
                candidates.append(base + ":" + parts[-1])
                candidates.append(base.replace(":", "_") + "_" + parts[-1])
    return tuple(sorted({value for value in candidates if re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{1,160}", value)}))


def validate_inject_command(
    command: object,
    watched: tuple[str, ...],
    reconnect_command: str | None,
) -> bool:
    """Allow only fixed governor commands and the session's watched reconnect."""
    if not isinstance(command, str) or len(command) > 256:
        return False
    if command in {"/compact", "/compact decider-local-only", "/reload-plugins"}:
        return True
    if command == reconnect_command:
        return True
    match = INJECT_RE.fullmatch(command)
    return bool(match and match.group(1) in watched)


def channel_process_patterns() -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Return safe process-name fragments used for launch-time attachment checks."""
    configured = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_IDLE_CHANNEL_PROCESS_PATTERNS", "")
    values = [value.strip() for value in configured.split(",") if value.strip()]
    return tuple(values or ["server.ts"])


def persistent_state_root() -> pathlib.Path:
    """Return the private, durable state root for post-mortem guard logs."""
    base = os.environ.get("XDG_STATE_HOME") or str(pathlib.Path.home() / ".local/state")
    root = pathlib.Path(base) / "claude-idle-compaction"
    safe_mkdir(root)
    return root


def proc_children(pid: int) -> list[int]:
    """Read one Linux process's direct children without invoking a scanner."""
    try:
        value = pathlib.Path(f"/proc/{pid}/task/{pid}/children").read_text()
    except OSError:
        return []
    return [int(child) for child in value.split() if child.isdigit()]


def process_command_line(pid: int) -> str:
    """Read a process command line for a local, non-secret attach check."""
    try:
        raw = pathlib.Path(f"/proc/{pid}/cmdline").read_bytes()
    except OSError:
        return ""
    return raw.replace(b"\0", b" ").decode("utf-8", "replace")


def expected_completion_transcript_entry(entry: dict) -> bool:
    """Ignore Claude's normal Stop bookkeeping when tailing the transcript."""
    return (
        (
            entry.get("type") == "system"
            and entry.get("subtype") in EXPECTED_COMPLETION_SYSTEM_SUBTYPES
        )
        or entry.get("type") in EXPECTED_COMPLETION_TRANSCRIPT_TYPES
    )


def transcript_entry_classification(entry: object) -> str:
    """Classify one JSONL record without retaining or exposing its contents."""
    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
        return "unknown"
    entry_type = entry.get("type")
    if entry_type == "user":
        if entry.get("isMeta") is True or entry.get("isSidechain") is True:
            return "internal-user-metadata"
        return "user-turn"
    if entry_type == "assistant":
        return "assistant-output"
    if entry_type == "attachment":
        return "internal-attachment"
    if isinstance(entry_type, str) and entry_type in TRANSCRIPT_INTERNAL_TYPES:
        return "internal-" + str(entry_type).replace("_", "-")
    if entry_type == "system":
        subtype = entry.get("subtype")
        if isinstance(subtype, str) and subtype in EXPECTED_COMPLETION_SYSTEM_SUBTYPES:
            return "internal-recap" if subtype == "away_summary" else "internal-completion"
        if isinstance(subtype, str) and subtype in TRANSCRIPT_INTERNAL_SYSTEM_SUBTYPES:
            return "internal-system"
        return "unknown"
    return "unknown"


def transcript_entry_timestamp(entry: dict) -> float | None:
    """Return an installed Claude JSONL timestamp without retaining content."""
    value = entry.get("timestamp")
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
        return None
    try:
        return datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00")).timestamp()
    except ValueError:
        return None


IDLE_SECONDS = {
    "long": env_int("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_SECONDS", 55 * 60),
    "short": env_int("CLAUDE_SHORT_CACHE_IDLE_COMPACT_SECONDS", 4 * 60),
}
TTL_SECONDS = {
    "long": env_int("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_LONG_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS", 60 * 60),
    "short": env_int("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_SHORT_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS", 5 * 60),
}


def validated_cache_decision(decision: object, provider: str, endpoint: str,
                             model: str, allow_observed: bool = False) -> dict:
    """Accept only the resolver's exact identity and ordered integer window."""
    if not isinstance(decision, dict):
        raise ValueError("cache-policy-not-object")
    mode, confidence = decision.get("mode"), decision.get("confidence")
    if mode == "fallback" and confidence == "fallback":
        # The caller owns fallback timing; stdout cannot redefine its profile.
        return decision
    if not (mode == "measured" and confidence == "supported" or
            allow_observed and mode == "observed" and confidence == "observed"):
        raise ValueError("cache-policy-confidence-invalid")
    def canonical(value: str) -> str:
        return value.strip().lower().replace("_", "-")
    expected_provider = canonical(provider)
    expected_provider = {"openai-codex": "openai",
                         "openai-codex-oauth": "openai"}.get(expected_provider, expected_provider)
    expected_endpoint = canonical(endpoint)
    if expected_provider == "openai" and expected_endpoint in {"openai", "openai-codex", "openai-codex-oauth"}:
        expected_endpoint = "openai-codex-oauth"
    expected_model = canonical(model)
    if expected_provider == "anthropic":
        expected_model = re.sub(r"-+", "-", re.sub(r"[ .]+", "-", expected_model)).strip("-")
        if not expected_model.startswith("claude-"):
            expected_model = "claude-" + expected_model
    for key, expected in (("provider", expected_provider), ("endpoint_id", expected_endpoint), ("model", expected_model)):
        if decision.get(key) != expected:
            raise ValueError("cache-policy-identity-mismatch")
    compact, expiry = decision.get("compact_before_seconds"), decision.get("cache_expires_seconds")
    if (type(compact) is not int or type(expiry) is not int or
            not 0 < compact < expiry or expiry > 2**31 - 1):
        raise ValueError("cache-policy-window-invalid")
    return decision


def measured_cache_window(model: str | None, profile: str) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    fallback_idle = IDLE_SECONDS[profile]
    fallback_ttl = TTL_SECONDS[profile]
    if not model:
        return fallback_idle, fallback_ttl, {"mode": "fallback", "reason": "model-unknown"}
    if any(os.environ.get(name) for name in (
        "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL", "ANTHROPIC_BEDROCK_BASE_URL",
        "CLAUDE_CODE_USE_BEDROCK", "CLAUDE_CODE_USE_VERTEX", "CLAUDE_CODE_USE_FOUNDRY",
    )):
        return fallback_idle, fallback_ttl, {"mode": "fallback", "reason": "endpoint-unknown"}
    helper = pathlib.Path(os.environ.get(
        "FLEET_CACHE_POLICY_HELPER", str(pathlib.Path.home() / "HelmCortex/FORGE/bin/cache-policy")
    )).expanduser()
    policy = pathlib.Path(os.environ.get(
        "FLEET_CACHE_POLICY_PATH",
        str(pathlib.Path.home() / "HelmCortex/ROOTS/DotRoot/agent-session/centre/data/cache-policy.json"),
    )).expanduser()
    try:
        completed = subprocess.run(
            [str(helper), "resolve", "--policy", str(policy), "--harness", "claude",
             "--provider", "anthropic", "--endpoint", "anthropic-default",
             "--model", model, "--fallback-compact", str(fallback_idle),
             "--fallback-cache", str(fallback_ttl)],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=True,
        )
        decision = validated_cache_decision(json.loads(completed.stdout), "anthropic", "anthropic-default", model)
        if decision["mode"] == "fallback":
            return fallback_idle, fallback_ttl, decision
        return decision["compact_before_seconds"], decision["cache_expires_seconds"], decision
    except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError, OverflowError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return fallback_idle, fallback_ttl, {"mode": "fallback", "reason": "resolver-error"}
MIN_TOKENS = env_int("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_MIN_TOKENS", 70000)
# Deterministic offline compaction (CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_MODE=deterministic).
# The pass runs on a COPY of the transcript, indexes every block it elides
# BEFORE eliding it, and never installs a session above the ceiling.  70_000 is
# the operator's hard post-compaction ceiling, not a target to approach.
OFFLINE_COMPACT_MODES = frozenset({"native", "deterministic"})
# Compactor verdicts that mean "nothing to do", not "the pass broke": they must
# never be answered with a paid /compact on a session that is already small.
OFFLINE_COMPACT_REFUSALS = frozenset({"no-elision-needed"})
OFFLINE_COMPACT_CEILING_TOKENS = env_int(
    "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_CEILING_TOKENS", 70_000, 1_000
)
OFFLINE_COMPACT_CHARS_PER_TOKEN = env_float(
    "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_CHARS_PER_TOKEN", 4.0, 0.5
)
OFFLINE_COMPACT_KEEP_RECENT_RECORDS = env_int(
    "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_KEEP_RECENT_RECORDS", 60, 0
)
OFFLINE_COMPACT_STUB_OVER_CHARS = env_int(
    "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_STUB_OVER_CHARS", 400, 80
)
# Pointers may point at an INDEX OF POINTERS: a run of elisions that belongs to
# one task collapses into one stub that carries a summary and names a group in
# the index.  It is on by default because a stub nobody can read is worse than
# the text it replaced, and the engine refuses a group that does not save space
# or cannot be expanded.
OFFLINE_COMPACT_GROUP_POINTERS = env_int("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_GROUP_POINTERS", 1, 0)
OFFLINE_COMPACT_GROUP_MIN_SAVING_CHARS = env_int(
    "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_GROUP_MIN_SAVING_CHARS", 400, 0
)
OFFLINE_COMPACT_PROJECT = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_PROJECT", "")
# Older PROSE (a human turn, an assistant turn, a tool call's arguments) may leave
# the working copy too -- as a pointer into the retained transcript, with the text
# byte-exact in the index.  Off by default: eliding tool output was always safe,
# moving a human word out of the working set is a decision, so it is opt-in per
# seat until the numbers are accepted.
OFFLINE_COMPACT_FOLD_PROSE = env_int("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_FOLD_PROSE", 0, 0)
OFFLINE_COMPACT_STOP_GRACE_SECONDS = env_float(
    "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_STOP_GRACE_SECONDS", 5.0, 0.1
)
# Ruling R-0032: the deterministic pass is due at the COLD CACHE BOUNDARY, not at
# a token threshold.  Normal operation keeps a large session and pays for it out
# of the provider prompt cache, so size is not a reason to compact; the risk
# being managed is re-ingesting a cold session of hundreds of thousands of
# tokens.  MIN_TOKENS above stays as a FLOOR ("do not bother with a tiny
# session"), never as the trigger.
OFFLINE_COMPACT_AGENT_FAMILY = (
    os.environ.get("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_AGENT_FAMILY", "claude").strip().lower() or "claude"
)
OFFLINE_COMPACT_SCOPE = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_SCOPE", "").strip().lower()
OFFLINE_COMPACT_HARNESS_INDEX_TOKENS = env_int(
    "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_HARNESS_INDEX_TOKENS", 0, 0
)
OFFLINE_COMPACT_HARNESS_OVERRIDE = env_int(
    "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_HARNESS_TOKENS", 0, 0
)


def offline_compact_due(
    *,
    status: str,
    idle_age: float | None,
    cache_idle_seconds: float | None,
    usage_limited: bool,
) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """Is the deterministic pass due?  The cold boundary, not the transcript size.

    A large session is the NORMAL state and is paid for out of the provider
    prompt cache, so size alone must never trigger a pass.  It is due when

      (a) the seat is idle and the measured cache window says the cache is at or
          past its compact-before boundary, or
      (b) the provider is usage-limited, so the paid path cannot run at all.

    If the cache is warm and the seat is active, doing nothing is CORRECT
    behaviour, not a skipped compaction -- the caller logs it as ``not-due`` and
    never as a failure.  Without a measurement the armed timer is the only
    evidence there is, and it has already fired: warmth is never invented.
    """
    if status == "active":
        return False, "seat-active"
    if usage_limited:
        return True, "usage-limited"
    if idle_age is None or cache_idle_seconds is None:
        return True, "cache-unmeasured"
    if idle_age < cache_idle_seconds:
        return False, "cache-warm"
    return True, "cache-cold"


def compaction_mode() -> str:
    """Return the compaction mode; an unrecognised value keeps today's native path."""
    value = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_MODE", "native").strip().lower()
    return value if value in OFFLINE_COMPACT_MODES else "native"


def argv_with_session(argv: list[str], session_id: str) -> list[str]:
    """Return argv that resumes exactly ``session_id``.

    Rewrites an existing ``--resume``/``-r`` value, replaces ``--continue``/``-c``
    (which cannot name a session), or inserts ``--resume <id>`` when the launcher
    named no session at all.  Only the offline-compaction swap calls this; the
    native path keeps the launcher's argv byte for byte.
    """
    out = list(argv)
    for index, token in enumerate(out):
        if token in {"--resume", "-r"} and index + 1 < len(out):
            out[index + 1] = session_id
            return out
        if token.startswith("--resume="):
            out[index] = "--resume=" + session_id
            return out
    for index, token in enumerate(out):
        if token in {"--continue", "-c"}:
            out[index:index + 1] = ["--resume", session_id]
            return out
    return ["--resume", session_id, *out]

# An unknown transcript record fails closed (transcript-uncertain), but the
# uncertainty must be a bounded caution, never a permanent veto: with the
# latch unexpired and hook Stops not arriving (dead control socket,
# 2026-08-30), one unknown record silenced compaction for two nights.  After
# this many seconds without cancellation evidence the gate self-heals and
# logs transcript-gate-expired.
TRANSCRIPT_GATE_MAX_SECONDS = env_int("CLAUDE_IDLE_TRANSCRIPT_GATE_MAX_SECONDS", 60 * 60, 60)
COMPACT_RETRY_SECONDS = env_int("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_RETRY_SECONDS", 5 * 60, 1)
COMPACT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = env_int("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS", 15 * 60, 1)
PTY_CHECK_ATTEMPTS = 3
PTY_CHECK_RETRY_SECONDS = 0.05
# Gate refusals that cannot clear without a new Stop, a control command, or
# a token-count change.  Every other refusal is transient (a PTY probe
# hiccup, activity that simply moved the idle window, a stale snapshot) and
# re-arms the timer instead of leaving the governor dormant.
DORMANT_GATE_REASONS = frozenset(
    {
        "session-disabled",
        "session-not-running",
        "already-compacting",
        "session-unbound",
        "no-normal-stop",
        "clean",
        "channel-recovery-pending",
        "below-token-threshold",

        "inject-command-refused",
        "unknown-injection-purpose",

)
SNAPSHOT_STOP_SKEW_SECONDS = env_float(

TRANSCRIPT_QUIESCENCE_SECONDS = env_float(

)
TRANSCRIPT_POLL_SECONDS = env_int(
    "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_TRANSCRIPT_POLL_SECONDS", 2, 1
)
INPUT_ESCAPE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = env_float(

)

LIFECYCLE_WAIT_SECONDS = env_int(

_development_channel_accept_window_seconds = env_float(

)

DEBUG_LOG_MAX_BYTES = env_int(
    "CLAUDE_IDLE_CHANNEL_DEBUG_MAX_BYTES", 2 * 1024 * 1024, 65536

TERMINAL_CLEANUP_BYTES = (
    b"\x1b[?1000l\x1b[?1002l\x1b[?1003l\x1b[?1004l"
    b"\x1b[?1005l\x1b[?1006l\x1b[?1015l"
    b"\x1b[?2004l\x1b[?25h\x1b[0m"
    # Kitty keyboard protocol: pop every pushed mode, then force legacy flags.
    # A child that dies before popping (e.g. an aborted startup) otherwise leaves
    # release/alternate-key reporting on, and CSI-u garbage leaks into fzf etc.
    b"\x1b[<99u\x1b[=0;1u"
)


def append_debug(path: pathlib.Path, message: str) -> None:
    """Append one prompt-free diagnostic to the session's private debug file."""
    if DEBUG:
        try:
            with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                stream.write(f"{now():.6f} {message}\n")
            os.chmod(path, 0o600)
        except OSError:
            pass


def short_id(value: object) -> str:
    """Return a non-sensitive truncated identifier for diagnostics."""
    text = str(value or "unknown")
    return text if len(text) <= 12 else text[:8] + "…"


def now() -> float:
    """Return wall-clock seconds used for cache-deadline comparisons."""
    return time.time()


def monotonic() -> float:
    """Return monotonic seconds used only for local retry/poll scheduling."""
    return time.monotonic()


def executable_generation() -> str:
    """Hash executable bytes without confusing install time with load time."""
    try:
        return hashlib.sha256(pathlib.Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    except OSError:
        return "unavailable"


# Capture once while this generation loads.  A later source/stow update can
# change the installed bytes, but it cannot rewrite this running process.
LOADED_GENERATION = executable_generation()


HERDR_SOURCE = "claude-warm"
HERDR_PANE_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{1,96}$")
HERDR_AGENT_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9 .:_-]{0,63}$")
HERDR_HEARTBEAT_SECONDS = env_float(
    "CLAUDE_WARM_HERDR_HEARTBEAT_SECONDS", 30.0, 5.0
)
HERDR_REGISTRATION_TTL_MS = env_int(
    "CLAUDE_WARM_HERDR_REGISTRATION_TTL_MS", 120000, 1000
)


class HerdrReporter:
    """Register identity and report PTY-derived state through Herdr's pane API."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        socket_path = os.environ.get("HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "")
        pane_id = os.environ.get("HERDR_PANE_ID", "")
        agent = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_WARM_HERDR_AGENT", "") or "Claude Code"
        self.socket_path = pathlib.Path(socket_path) if socket_path else None
        self.pane_id = pane_id if HERDR_PANE_RE.fullmatch(pane_id) else None
        self.agent = agent if HERDR_AGENT_RE.fullmatch(agent) else None
        self.enabled = (
            os.environ.get("HERDR_ENV") == "1"
            and self.socket_path is not None
            and self.socket_path.is_absolute()
            and len(str(self.socket_path)) < 100
            and self.pane_id is not None
            and self.agent is not None
        )
        self.source = HERDR_SOURCE
        self.sequence = 0
        self.registered = False
        self.last_report_at: float | None = None
        self.last_report_state: str | None = None
        self.last_error: str | None = None
        self.next_heartbeat = 0.0
        self.child_pid: int | None = None
        self.agent_session_id: str | None = None

    def state(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        """Return prompt-free Herdr ownership state for the supervisor state file."""
        return {
            "herdr_enabled": self.enabled,
            "herdr_pane_id": self.pane_id,
            "herdr_agent": self.agent,
            "herdr_source": self.source,
            "herdr_registered": self.registered,
            "herdr_last_report_at": self.last_report_at,
            "herdr_last_report_state": self.last_report_state,
            "herdr_last_error": self.last_error,
            "herdr_child_pid": self.child_pid,
            "herdr_agent_session_id": self.agent_session_id,
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _valid_session_id(value: object) -> str | None:
        if isinstance(value, str) and SESSION_RE.fullmatch(value):
            return value
        return None

    def _send(self, method: str, params: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
        """Send one bounded JSON-RPC request to the inherited Herdr socket."""
        if not self.enabled or self.socket_path is None:
            return False
        self.sequence += 1
        request = {
            "id": f"{self.source}:{self.sequence}",
            "method": method,
            "params": params,
        }
        try:
            with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as connection:
                connection.settimeout(0.5)
                connection.connect(str(self.socket_path))
                connection.sendall((json.dumps(request, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n").encode())
                response = connection.recv(4096)
            if response:
                decoded = json.loads(response.decode("utf-8"))
                if isinstance(decoded, dict) and decoded.get("error"):
                    error = decoded["error"]
                    code = error.get("code") if isinstance(error, dict) else "request-failed"
                    self.last_error = str(code)[:96]
                    return False
            return True
        except (OSError, ValueError, UnicodeError) as error:
            self.last_error = type(error).__name__
            return False

    def _base(self, session_id: object = None) -> dict[str, object]:
        """Return validated Herdr identity fields, including resumed session ID."""
        safe_session_id = self._valid_session_id(session_id)
        base = {
            "pane_id": self.pane_id,
            "source": self.source,
            "agent": self.agent,
        }
        if safe_session_id:
            base["agent_session_id"] = safe_session_id
        return base

    def _metadata(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        """Return display identity and bounded supervisor/child PID metadata."""
        tokens = {
            "supervisor_pid": str(os.getpid()),
            "pty_owner": "claude-warm",
        }
        if isinstance(self.child_pid, int) and self.child_pid > 0:
            tokens["claude_pid"] = str(self.child_pid)
        return {
            "pane_id": self.pane_id,
            "source": self.source,
            "agent": self.agent,
            "display_agent": self.agent,
            "tokens": tokens,
            "ttl_ms": HERDR_REGISTRATION_TTL_MS,
        }

    def register(self, session_id: object = None) -> None:
        """Refresh identity and metadata without sending a visible state."""
        if not self.enabled:
            return
        safe_session_id = self._valid_session_id(session_id)
        base = self._base(safe_session_id)
        if safe_session_id and safe_session_id != self.agent_session_id:
            self._send("pane.report_agent_session", dict(base))
            self.agent_session_id = safe_session_id
        success = self._send("pane.report_metadata", self._metadata())
        self.registered = success or self.registered
        self.last_report_at = now()
        self.next_heartbeat = monotonic() + HERDR_HEARTBEAT_SECONDS

    def report_state(self, state: str, session_id: object = None) -> None:
        """Report only a state derived from PTY output, never from a hook event."""
        if not self.enabled or state not in {"idle", "working", "blocked", "unknown"}:
            return
        self.register(session_id)
        report = self._base(session_id)
        report["state"] = state
        self._send("pane.report_agent", report)
        self.last_report_state = state
        self.last_report_at = now()
        self.next_heartbeat = monotonic() + HERDR_HEARTBEAT_SECONDS

    def heartbeat(self, state: str | None = None, session_id: object = None) -> None:
        """Refresh metadata, optionally repeating the last PTY-derived state."""
        if self.enabled and monotonic() >= self.next_heartbeat:
            if state in {"idle", "working", "blocked", "unknown"}:
                self.report_state(state, session_id)
            else:
                self.register(session_id)

    def release(self) -> None:
        """Remove this supervisor's Herdr registration on every orderly exit."""
        if not self.enabled or self.socket_path is None:
            return
        params = {
            "pane_id": self.pane_id,
            "source": self.source,
            "agent": self.agent,
        }
        if self._send("pane.release_agent", params):
            self.registered = False
        self.last_report_state = "released"
        self.last_report_at = now()


HERDR_SCREEN_BYTES = 128 * 1024


class TerminalScreenModel:
    """Bounded attributed cells for the PTY owner's prompt-box checks.

    This mirrors cursor/erase operations used by Ink, including redraws that
    omit the prompt glyph. It never changes the proxied bytes. Unsupported
    mutations fail closed until a full screen clear; incomplete UTF-8/escapes
    cannot establish an empty prompt.
    """

    MAX_ROWS = 256
    MAX_COLUMNS = 1024
    ESCAPE_RE = re.compile(
        r"\x1b(?:\[[0-?]*[ -/]*[@-~]|\][^\x07\x1b]*(?:\x07|\x1b\\)|"
        r"[P^_].*?\x1b\\|[()][0-2A-Z]|[78]|[@-OQ-Za-z\\])", re.DOTALL
    )

    def __init__(self):
        self.reset()

    def reset(self):
        self.cells = {}
        self.row = self.column = 0
        self.saved_cursor = (0, 0)
        self.faint = self.bold = self.inverse = False
        self.foreground = None
        self.pending = ""
        self.decoder = codecs.getincrementaldecoder("utf-8")("replace")
        self.uncertain = False
        self.alternate_screen = False

    def _sgr(self, body):
        try:
            values = [int(value or "0") for value in body.split(";")]
        except ValueError:
            self.uncertain = True
            return
        index = 0
        while index < len(values):
            code = values[index]
            index += 1
            if code == 0:
                self.faint = self.bold = self.inverse = False
                self.foreground = None
            elif code == 1:
                self.bold = True
            elif code == 2:
                self.faint = True
            elif code == 22:
                self.faint = self.bold = False
            elif code in {7, 27}:
                self.inverse = code == 7
            elif code == 39:
                self.foreground = None
            elif 30 <= code <= 37 or 90 <= code <= 97:
                self.foreground = ("ansi", code)
            elif code in {38, 48, 58}:
                # Consume the WHOLE colour payload: RGB/index values of 2
                # are colours, never a faint-intensity toggle.
                mode = values[index] if index < len(values) else None
                size = 1 if mode == 5 else 3 if mode == 2 else 0
                if not size or index + size >= len(values):
                    self.uncertain = True
                    return
                components = values[index + 1:index + size + 1]
                if any(not 0 <= value <= 255 for value in components):
                    self.uncertain = True
                    return
                if code == 38:
                    self.foreground = ("indexed", *components) if mode == 5 else ("rgb", *components)
                index += size + 1

    def _erase(self, predicate):
        self.cells = {position: cell for position, cell in self.cells.items()
                      if not predicate(*position)}

    def _escape(self, sequence):
        if not sequence.startswith("\x1b["):
            if sequence == "\x1b7":
                self.saved_cursor = (self.row, self.column)
            elif sequence == "\x1b8":
                self.row, self.column = self.saved_cursor
            elif not sequence.startswith(("\x1b]", "\x1b(", "\x1b)")):
                # OSC metadata and charset selection do not paint cells.
                # Unknown non-CSI mutations (e.g. reverse index) must veto.
                self.uncertain = True
            return
        body, final = sequence[2:-1], sequence[-1]
        if body == "?1049" and final in {"h", "l"}:
            if final == "h" and not self.alternate_screen:
                self.cells.clear()
                self.row = self.column = 0
            elif final == "l" and self.alternate_screen:
                # The restored primary buffer is not in this bounded model.
                # Require an explicit clear/redraw before trusting its cells.
                self.uncertain = True
            self.alternate_screen = final == "h"
            return
        if final == "m":
            # Private keyboard negotiation (>4m) is not SGR.
            if not body.startswith((">", "?")):
                self._sgr(body)
            return
        if body.startswith(("?", ">", "<", "=")) or final in {"h", "l", "n", "c", "q", "t", "r"}:
            return
        try:
            values = [int(value or "0") for value in body.split(";")]
        except ValueError:
            self.uncertain = True
            return
        amount = values[0] or 1
        if final in {"H", "f"}:
            self.row = amount - 1
            self.column = (values[1] or 1) - 1 if len(values) > 1 else 0
        elif final in {"G", "`"}:
            self.column = amount - 1
        elif final == "d":
            self.row = amount - 1
        elif final in {"A", "B", "C", "D", "E", "F"}:
            if final in {"A", "F"}: self.row -= amount
            if final in {"B", "E"}: self.row += amount
            if final == "C": self.column += amount
            if final == "D": self.column -= amount
            if final in {"E", "F"}: self.column = 0
        elif final == "K":
            mode = values[0]
            self._erase(lambda row, column: row == self.row and (
                mode == 2 or mode == 0 and column >= self.column
                or mode == 1 and column <= self.column))
        elif final == "J":
            mode = values[0]
            if mode in {2, 3}:
                self.cells.clear()
                self.uncertain = False
            else:
                self._erase(lambda row, column: (
                    mode == 0 and (row, column) >= (self.row, self.column)
                    or mode == 1 and (row, column) <= (self.row, self.column)))
        elif final == "s":
            self.saved_cursor = (self.row, self.column)
        elif final == "u":
            self.row, self.column = self.saved_cursor
        else:
            self.uncertain = True
        self.row = max(0, self.row)
        self.column = max(0, self.column)
        if self.row >= self.MAX_ROWS or self.column >= self.MAX_COLUMNS:
            self.uncertain = True
            self.row = min(self.row, self.MAX_ROWS - 1)
            self.column = min(self.column, self.MAX_COLUMNS - 1)

    def feed(self, data):
        text = self.pending + self.decoder.decode(data)
        self.pending = ""
        index = 0
        while index < len(text):
            character = text[index]
            if character == "\x1b":
                match = self.ESCAPE_RE.match(text, index)
                if match is None:
                    self.pending = text[index:]
                    if len(self.pending) > 2048:
                        self.pending = ""
                        self.uncertain = True
                    break
                self._escape(match.group())
                index = match.end()
                continue
            index += 1
            if character == "\r":
                self.column = 0
            elif character == "\n":
                self.row += 1
                if self.row == self.MAX_ROWS:
                    self.cells = {(row - 1, column): cell
                                  for (row, column), cell in self.cells.items() if row > 0}
                    self.row -= 1
            elif character == "\t":
                self.column = min(self.MAX_COLUMNS - 1, (self.column // 8 + 1) * 8)
            elif character == "\b":
                self.column = max(0, self.column - 1)
            elif ord(character) >= 0x20:
                width = 0 if unicodedata.combining(character) else (
                    2 if unicodedata.east_asian_width(character) in {"W", "F"} else 1)
                if width == 0:
                    previous = self.cells.get((self.row, self.column - 1))
                    if previous:
                        self.cells[self.row, self.column - 1] = (previous[0] + character, *previous[1:])
                    continue
                self.cells[self.row, self.column] = (
                    character, self.faint, self.bold, self.inverse, self.foreground)
                for offset in range(1, width):
                    self.cells[self.row, self.column + offset] = ("", False, False, False, None)
                self.column += width
                if self.column >= self.MAX_COLUMNS:
                    self.uncertain = True
                    self.column = self.MAX_COLUMNS - 1

    def lines(self):
        rows = {}
        for (row, column), cell in self.cells.items():
            rows.setdefault(row, {})[column] = cell
        blank = (" ", False, False, False, None)
        return [(row, [columns.get(column, blank) for column in range(max(columns) + 1)])
                for row, columns in sorted(rows.items())]

    def visible_text(self):
        return "\n".join("".join(cell[0] for cell in cells) for _row, cells in self.lines())

    def complete(self):
        return not self.uncertain and not self.pending and not self.decoder.getstate()[0]


class ClaudeScreenClassifier:
    """Classify visible Claude PTY output without consuming or rewriting it."""

    OSC_TITLE_RE = re.compile(
        r"\x1b\](?:0|1|2);(.*?)(?:\x07|\x1b\\)", re.DOTALL
    )
    ANSI_RE = re.compile(
        r"\x1b(?:\[[0-?]*[ -/]*[@-~]|\][^\x07\x1b]*(?:\x07|\x1b\\)|[@-_])"
    )
    WORKING_RE = re.compile(
        r"^[ \t]*[✻✳✽✶✷]\s+Working(?:…|\.\.\.)?(?:\s|$)", re.MULTILINE
    )
    PROMPT_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*❯(?:\s.*)?$", re.MULTILINE)
    STRONG_BLOCKERS = (
        "enter to confirm",
        "enter to select",
        "enter to submit",
        "esc to cancel",
        "do you want to proceed?",
        "would you like to proceed?",
        "allow command?",
    )

    # Attribute values are a renderer profile, never suggestion wording.
    GHOST_FOREGROUNDS = frozenset({("ansi", 90), ("indexed", 8), ("rgb", 153, 153, 153)})

    def __init__(self, *, prompt_glyph="❯", ghost_foregrounds=None, prompt_border="─") -> None:
        self.raw = bytearray()
        self.osc_title = ""
        self.state: str | None = None
        self.screen = TerminalScreenModel()
        self.prompt_glyph = prompt_glyph
        self.prompt_border = prompt_border
        self.ghost_foregrounds = (self.GHOST_FOREGROUNDS if ghost_foregrounds is None
                                  else frozenset(ghost_foregrounds))
        self.PROMPT_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*" + re.escape(prompt_glyph) + r"(?:\s.*)?$", re.MULTILINE)

    def reset(self) -> None:
        """Forget the previous screen when Claude binds a new session."""
        self.raw.clear()
        self.osc_title = ""
        self.state = None
        self.screen.reset()

    def _visible_text(self) -> str:
        return self.screen.visible_text()

    def prompt_input_state(self, *, permission_selection=False) -> str:
        """Return empty/typed/unknown for the final rendered prompt box.

        Faint or profile-grey text is a display hint. Normal-intensity cells
        always constitute input, regardless of their words. A software cursor
        may invert the first hint cell without faint: recognize that captured
        shape only at the actual cursor, with an otherwise wholly muted hint.
        The owner must still veto observed local input before using this hint.
        """
        if not self.screen.complete():
            return "unknown"
        lines = self.screen.lines()
        prompts = [index for index, (_row, cells) in enumerate(lines)
                   if self.PROMPT_RE.fullmatch("".join(cell[0] for cell in cells))]
        # A permission dialog's selected Yes/No row is a menu cursor, not
        # an editable draft. Ignore only these exact labels, only when the
        # caller has validated a permission dialog and the model is blocked.
        dialog = permission_selection and self._classify(self._visible_text()) == "blocked"
        if dialog:
            selection = re.compile(r"\s*❯\s*[123]\.\s*(?:Yes|Yes, and don't ask again|No)\s*")
            if self.screen.alternate_screen:
                selection = re.compile(
                    r"\s*❯\s*[1-4]\.\s*(?:Yes|Yes, and don't ask again|No|"
                    r"Yes, and always allow access to .+|"
                    r"Yes, and switch to auto mode · auto mode handles these prompts for you)\s*")
                selected = [index for index in prompts
                            if selection.fullmatch("".join(cell[0] for cell in lines[index][1]))]
                questions = [index for index, (_row, cells) in enumerate(lines)
                             if any(marker in "".join(cell[0] for cell in cells).lower()
                                    for marker in ("do you want to proceed?", "would you like to proceed?", "allow command?"))]
                if len(selected) != 1 or not questions or selected[0] <= questions[-1]:
                    return "unknown"
                menu_row, menu_cells = lines[selected[0]]
                menu_column = next(i for i, cell in enumerate(menu_cells) if cell[0] == self.prompt_glyph)
                if (self.screen.row, self.screen.column) != (menu_row, menu_column):
                    return "unknown"
                # Fullscreen keeps submitted prompts above its modal. Only
                # the validated question/selection/cancel layout scopes them
                # out; an editable row inside the modal still vetoes a deny.
                if not any("esc to cancel" in "".join(c[0] for c in cells).lower()
                           for _row, cells in lines[selected[0] + 1:]):
                    return "unknown"
                prompts = [index for index in prompts if index > questions[-1]]
            prompts = [index for index in prompts
                       if not selection.fullmatch("".join(cell[0] for cell in lines[index][1]))]
        if not prompts:
            return "empty" if dialog else "unknown"
        at = prompts[-1]
        row, cells = lines[at]
        glyph_at = next(index for index, cell in enumerate(cells) if cell[0] == self.prompt_glyph)
        content = [(row, column, cell) for column, cell in enumerate(cells)
                   if column > glyph_at and cell[0].strip()]
        for next_row, next_cells in lines[at + 1:]:
            text = "".join(cell[0] for cell in next_cells).strip()
            if len(text) >= 3 and set(text) == {self.prompt_border}:
                # Only the painted input box ends the prompt; arbitrary dim
                # transcript/footer text cannot excuse a normal draft cell.
                break
            content.extend((next_row, column, cell) for column, cell in enumerate(next_cells)
                           if cell[0].strip())
        if not content:
            return "empty"
        def muted(cell):
            _text, faint, bold, _inverse, foreground = cell
            return not bold and (faint or foreground in self.ghost_foregrounds)
        if all(muted(cell) for _row, _column, cell in content):
            return "empty"
        first_row, first_column, first = content[0]
        if (len(content) > 1 and first[3] and not first[2]
                and (self.screen.row, self.screen.column) == (first_row, first_column)
                and all(muted(cell) for _row, _column, cell in content[1:])):
            return "empty"
        return "typed"

    def _classify(self, visible: str) -> str:
        recent = visible[-HERDR_SCREEN_BYTES:]
        lowered = recent.lower()
        lines = [line.rstrip() for line in recent.splitlines() if line.strip()]
        bottom = "\n".join(lines[-8:])
        blocker_positions = [
            lowered.rfind(marker)
            for marker in self.STRONG_BLOCKERS
            if marker in lowered
        ]
        last_blocker = max(blocker_positions, default=-1)
        working_matches = list(self.WORKING_RE.finditer(recent))
        last_working = working_matches[-1].start() if working_matches else -1
        prompt_matches = list(self.PROMPT_RE.finditer(recent))
        last_prompt = prompt_matches[-1].start() if prompt_matches else -1
        # The PTY stream is not a full terminal emulator.  Treat the newest
        # strong cue as authoritative so a later prompt can clear an older
        # approval screen after Claude redraws it with erase/cursor bytes.
        if last_blocker >= 0 and last_blocker >= max(last_working, last_prompt):
            return "blocked"
        if self.screen.alternate_screen and "esc to interrupt" in bottom.lower():
            # Fullscreen leaves the empty input box beneath its spinner.
            # Its captured interrupt footer is the active-turn cue.
            return "working"
        if last_working >= 0 and (
            last_working >= last_prompt
            or (last_prompt < 0 and (
            "esc to interrupt" in lowered and "working" in lowered
            ))
        ):
            return "working"
        if last_prompt >= 0 and self.PROMPT_RE.search(bottom):
            return "idle"
        return "unknown"

    def feed(self, data: bytes) -> str | None:
        """Consume a bounded copy for classification and return state changes."""
        if not data:
            return None
        self.screen.feed(data)
        self.raw.extend(data)
        if len(self.raw) > HERDR_SCREEN_BYTES:
            del self.raw[:-HERDR_SCREEN_BYTES]
        titles = list(self.OSC_TITLE_RE.finditer(bytes(self.raw).decode("utf-8", "replace")))
        if titles:
            self.osc_title = titles[-1].group(1)
        state = self._classify(self._visible_text())
        if state == self.state:
            return None
        self.state = state
        return state


class DevelopmentChannelPromptMatcher:
    """Recognize only Claude Code 2.1.291's complete development dialog."""

    BUFFER_BYTES = 64 * 1024
    TITLE = "WARNING: Loading development channels"
    WARNING = (
        "--dangerously-load-development-channels is for local channel "
        "development only. Do not use this option to run channels you have "
        "downloaded off the internet."
    )
    APPROVED_CHANNELS_HELP = (
        "Please use --channels to run a list of approved channels."
    )
    CONFIRM_LABEL = "I am using this for local development"
    EXIT_LABEL = "Exit"
    CHANNELS_RE = re.compile(
        rf"Channels:\s*(?P<entries>{DEVELOPMENT_CHANNEL_ENTRY_PATTERN}"
        rf"(?:\s*,\s*{DEVELOPMENT_CHANNEL_ENTRY_PATTERN})*)"
    )

    def __init__(self, opted_in: tuple[str, ...]):
        self.opted_in = frozenset(opted_in)
        self.raw = bytearray()
        self.matched = False

    def feed(self, data: bytes) -> tuple[str, ...] | None:
        """Return the exact approved entries once, or fail closed."""
        if self.matched or not self.opted_in or not data:
            return None
        self.raw.extend(data)
        if len(self.raw) > self.BUFFER_BYTES:
            del self.raw[:-self.BUFFER_BYTES]
        visible = ClaudeScreenClassifier.ANSI_RE.sub(
            "", bytes(self.raw).decode("utf-8", "replace")
        )
        visible = "".join(
            character
            for character in visible
            if character in "\n\r\t" or ord(character) >= 0x20
        )
        normalized = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", visible)
        title_at = normalized.rfind(self.TITLE)
        if title_at < 0:
            return None
        candidate = normalized[title_at:]
        position = 0
        for marker in (self.TITLE, self.WARNING, self.APPROVED_CHANNELS_HELP):
            found = candidate.find(marker, position)
            if found < 0:
                return None
            position = found + len(marker)
        channels = self.CHANNELS_RE.search(candidate, position)
        if channels is None:
            return None
        confirm_at = candidate.find(self.CONFIRM_LABEL, channels.end())
        exit_at = candidate.find(
            self.EXIT_LABEL,
            confirm_at + len(self.CONFIRM_LABEL) if confirm_at >= 0 else 0,
        )
        if confirm_at < 0 or exit_at < 0:
            return None
        # If a future renderer adds an entry shape this version does not know,
        # its server:/plugin: prefix remains between the parsed list and the
        # affirmative label.  Refuse rather than accepting a partial list.
        remainder = candidate[channels.end():confirm_at]
        if "server:" in remainder or "plugin:" in remainder:
            return None
        prompted = tuple(
            entry.strip() for entry in channels.group("entries").split(",")
        )
        if not prompted or any(entry not in self.opted_in for entry in prompted):
            return None
        self.matched = True
        return prompted


class TerminalInputClassifier:
    """Classify raw outer-terminal input without suppressing any forwarded byte."""

    PASTE_START = b"\x1b[200~"
    PASTE_END = b"\x1b[201~"
    C1_PASTE_START = b"\x9b200~"
    C1_PASTE_END = b"\x9b201~"

    def __init__(self, escape_timeout: float = INPUT_ESCAPE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS):
        self.escape_timeout = escape_timeout
        self.buffer = bytearray()
        self.in_bracketed_paste = False
        self.paste_end_marker: bytes | None = None
        self.pending_since: float | None = None

    @staticmethod
    def _event(kind: str, length: int, human: bool) -> tuple[str, int, bool]:
        return kind, length, human

    @staticmethod
    def _suffix_prefix_length(value: bytes, marker: bytes) -> int:
        maximum = min(len(value), len(marker) - 1)
        for length in range(maximum, 0, -1):
            if value[-length:] == marker[:length]:
                return length
        return 0

    def _drain_paste(self, events: list[tuple[str, int, bool]]) -> bool:
        marker = self.paste_end_marker or self.PASTE_END
        raw = bytes(self.buffer)
        marker_index = raw.find(marker)
        if marker_index >= 0:
            if marker_index:
                events.append(self._event("bracketed-paste-payload", marker_index, True))
                del self.buffer[:marker_index]
            del self.buffer[: len(marker)]
            events.append(self._event("bracketed-paste-end", len(marker), False))
            self.in_bracketed_paste = False
            self.paste_end_marker = None
            return True
        suffix = self._suffix_prefix_length(raw, marker)
        payload_length = len(raw) - suffix
        if payload_length:
            events.append(self._event("bracketed-paste-payload", payload_length, True))
            del self.buffer[:payload_length]
            return True
        return False

    def _classify_csi(self, sequence: bytes, prefix_length: int) -> tuple[str, bool]:
        body = sequence[prefix_length:-1]
        final = sequence[-1:]
        if sequence in {self.PASTE_START, self.C1_PASTE_START}:
            self.in_bracketed_paste = True
            self.paste_end_marker = self.PASTE_END if sequence == self.PASTE_START else self.C1_PASTE_END
            return "bracketed-paste-start", False
        if sequence in {self.PASTE_END, self.C1_PASTE_END}:
            self.in_bracketed_paste = False
            self.paste_end_marker = None
            return "bracketed-paste-end", False
        if final in {b"M", b"m"} and body.startswith(b"<"):
            return "sgr-mouse-report", False
        if not body and final in {b"I", b"O"}:
            return "focus-report", False
        if final == b"R" and re.fullmatch(rb"\d+;\d+", body):
            return "cursor-position-report", False
        if final == b"n":
            return "device-status-report", False
        if final == b"c" and (not body or body[:1] in {b"?", b">", b"!", b"="}):
            return "device-attribute-report", False
        if final == b"y" and body.startswith(b"?"):
            return "terminal-negotiation", False
        if final == b"t" and re.fullmatch(rb"[0-9;]+", body):
            return "terminal-negotiation", False
        if final == b"u":
            return "keyboard-csi-u", True
        return "keyboard-csi" if final else "unknown-input", True

    def _drain(self) -> list[tuple[str, int, bool]]:
        events: list[tuple[str, int, bool]] = []
        while self.buffer:
            if self.in_bracketed_paste:
                if not self._drain_paste(events):
                    break
                continue
            first = self.buffer[0]
            if first not in {0x1B, 0x9B, 0x9D, 0x90, 0x98, 0x9E, 0x9F}:
                protocol_markers = [
                    marker
                    for marker in (0x1B, 0x9B, 0x9D, 0x90, 0x98, 0x9E, 0x9F)
                    if marker in self.buffer
                ]
                length = min(self.buffer.index(marker) for marker in protocol_markers) if protocol_markers else len(self.buffer)
                del self.buffer[:length]
                events.append(self._event("keyboard-input", length, True))
                continue
            if first == 0x9B:
                prefix_length = 1
                if len(self.buffer) < 2:
                    break
                final_index = next(
                    (index for index, value in enumerate(self.buffer[1:], 1) if 0x40 <= value <= 0x7E),
                    None,
                )
                if final_index is None:
                    break
                sequence = bytes(self.buffer[: final_index + 1])
                kind, human = self._classify_csi(sequence, prefix_length)
                del self.buffer[: final_index + 1]
                events.append(self._event(kind, len(sequence), human))
                continue
            if len(self.buffer) < 2:
                break
            if self.buffer[:2] == b"\x1b[":
                if len(self.buffer) == 2:
                    break
                if self.buffer[2:3] == b"M":
                    if len(self.buffer) < 6:
                        break
                    del self.buffer[:6]
                    events.append(self._event("legacy-mouse-report", 6, False))
                    continue
                final_index = next(
                    (index for index, value in enumerate(self.buffer[2:], 2) if 0x40 <= value <= 0x7E),
                    None,
                )
                if final_index is None:
                    break
                sequence = bytes(self.buffer[: final_index + 1])
                kind, human = self._classify_csi(sequence, 2)
                del self.buffer[: final_index + 1]
                events.append(self._event(kind, len(sequence), human))
                continue
            if bytes(self.buffer[:2]) in {b"\x1b]", b"\x1bP", b"\x1b^", b"\x1b_"}:
                bell = self.buffer.find(b"\x07") if self.buffer[1] == 0x5D else -1
                string_terminator = self.buffer.find(b"\x1b\\")
                terminators = [value for value in (bell, string_terminator) if value >= 0]
                if not terminators:
                    break
                terminator = min(terminators)
                length = terminator + (1 if bell == terminator else 2)
                del self.buffer[:length]
                events.append(self._event("terminal-negotiation", length, False))
                continue
            if self.buffer[0] == 0x9D:
                terminator = self.buffer.find(b"\x07")
                if terminator < 0:
                    break
                length = terminator + 1
                del self.buffer[:length]
                events.append(self._event("terminal-negotiation", length, False))
                continue
            if self.buffer[0] in {0x90, 0x98, 0x9E, 0x9F}:
                terminator = self.buffer.find(b"\x9c")
                if terminator < 0:
                    break
                length = terminator + 1
                del self.buffer[:length]
                events.append(self._event("terminal-negotiation", length, False))
                continue
            if self.buffer[:2] == b"\x1bO":
                if len(self.buffer) < 3:
                    break
                del self.buffer[:3]
                events.append(self._event("keyboard-ss3", 3, True))
                continue
            del self.buffer[:2]
            events.append(self._event("unknown-input", 2, True))
        return events

    def feed(self, data: bytes) -> list[tuple[str, int, bool]]:
        if not data:
            return []
        self.buffer.extend(data)
        if self.pending_since is None and self.buffer and self.buffer[0] in {0x1B, 0x9B, 0x9D, 0x90, 0x98, 0x9E, 0x9F}:
            self.pending_since = monotonic()
        events = self._drain()
        if self.buffer and self.pending_since is None:
            self.pending_since = monotonic()
        if not self.buffer:
            self.pending_since = None
        return events

    def flush_expired(self) -> list[tuple[str, int, bool]]:
        if not self.buffer or self.pending_since is None:
            return []
        if monotonic() - self.pending_since < self.escape_timeout:
            return []
        length = len(self.buffer)
        self.buffer.clear()
        self.pending_since = None
        self.in_bracketed_paste = False
        self.paste_end_marker = None
        return [self._event("unknown-input", length, True)]


def atomic_json(path: pathlib.Path, payload: dict) -> None:
    """Write PAYLOAD atomically with owner-only permissions."""
    temporary = path.with_name(f".{path.name}.tmp.{os.getpid()}")
    data = json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n"
    fd = os.open(temporary, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            stream.write(data)
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.replace(temporary, path)
    finally:
        try:
            temporary.unlink()
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass


def safe_mkdir(path: pathlib.Path) -> None:
    """Create PATH as a private directory."""
    path.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    os.chmod(path, 0o700)


def effective_profile(requested: str | None) -> str:
    """Resolve a requested Claude cache profile without guessing backend TTL."""
    requested = requested or os.environ.get("CLAUDE_IDLE_CACHE_PROFILE", "auto")
    if requested in {"long", "short"}:
        return requested
    force_short = os.environ.get("FORCE_PROMPT_CACHING_5M", "").lower()
    force_long = os.environ.get("ENABLE_PROMPT_CACHING_1H", "").lower()
    if force_short in {"1", "true", "yes"}:
        return "short"
    if force_long in {"1", "true", "yes"}:
        return "long"
    return "long"


def runtime_root() -> pathlib.Path:
    """Return the required XDG runtime root or fail before launching Claude."""
    value = os.environ.get("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR")
    if not value:
        raise RuntimeError("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR is required for supervised Claude sessions")
    root = pathlib.Path(value) / "claude-idle-compaction"
    safe_mkdir(root)
    return root


def resolve_claude() -> str:
    """Resolve the real Claude executable without recursively selecting this wrapper."""
    explicit = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_IDLE_REAL_EXECUTABLE")
    candidates = [explicit] if explicit else []
    found = shutil.which("claude")
    if found:
        candidates.append(found)
    candidates.extend(
        [
            str(pathlib.Path.home() / ".local/bin/claude"),
            str(pathlib.Path.home() / ".npm-global/bin/claude"),
        ]
    )
    wrapper = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve()
    for candidate in candidates:
        if not candidate:
            continue
        path = pathlib.Path(candidate).expanduser()
        try:
            resolved = path.resolve()
        except OSError:
            continue
        if resolved == wrapper or not resolved.is_file() or not os.access(resolved, os.X_OK):
            continue
        if resolved.name in {"claude-warm", "claude-warmctl"}:
            continue
        return str(resolved)
    raise RuntimeError("real Claude executable not found; set CLAUDE_IDLE_REAL_EXECUTABLE")


def child_alive(pid: int | None) -> bool:
    """Return whether PID is still a live process."""
    if not pid:
        return False
    try:
        os.kill(pid, 0)
        return True
    except OSError:
        return False



def _hold_guardian_module():
    """Reuse the mail-wake guardian and pending-input checks for plain owners."""
    import importlib.util
    name = "_pty_hold_guardian"
    if name not in sys.modules:
        path = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().with_name("mailcortex_claude.py")
        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
