    def _save_preservation(self) -> None:
        """Persist before acknowledgement so lost replies are safely replayable."""
        path = self._preservation_path()
        if path is None or self.preservation_request_id is None:
            return
        payload = {
            "session_id": self.session_id,
            "request_id": self.preservation_request_id,
            "reservation_id": self.preservation_reservation_id,
            "state": self.preservation_state,
            "failure": self.preservation_failure,
            "expected_epoch": self.preservation_expected_epoch,
        }
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".tmp")
        temporary.write_text(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
        os.chmod(temporary, 0o600)
        os.replace(temporary, path)

    def _load_preservation(self) -> None:
        """Recover terminal state; in-flight work requires a fresh owner request."""
        self.preservation_request_id = None
        self.preservation_reservation_id = None
        self.preservation_state = None
        self.preservation_failure = None
        self.preservation_expected_epoch = None
        path = self._preservation_path()
        if path is None:
            return
        try:
            payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            return
        if payload.get("session_id") != self.session_id:
            return
        self.preservation_request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        self.preservation_reservation_id = payload.get("reservation_id")
        self.preservation_state = payload.get("state")
        self.preservation_failure = payload.get("failure")
        self.preservation_expected_epoch = payload.get("expected_epoch")
        if self.preservation_state in {"reserved", "requested", "native_started"}:
            self.preservation_state = "pending_recovery"
            self.preservation_failure = "owner-restarted"
            self._save_preservation()

    def _request_preservation(self, message: dict) -> dict:
        """Persist one idempotent request and compact only through this PTY owner."""
        request_id = message.get("request_id")
        reservation_id = message.get("reservation_id")
        expected_session_id = message.get("expected_session_id")
        expected_supervisor_pid = message.get("expected_supervisor_pid")
        expected_epoch = message.get("expected_epoch")
        if not all(
            isinstance(value, str) and SESSION_RE.fullmatch(value)
            for value in (request_id, reservation_id, expected_session_id)
        ):
            return {"ok": False, "error": "invalid-preservation-identity"}
        if not isinstance(expected_supervisor_pid, int) or not isinstance(expected_epoch, int):
            return {"ok": False, "error": "invalid-preservation-owner"}
        if self.preservation_request_id == request_id:
            return {"ok": True, "replayed": True, "state": self.state()}
        if self.preservation_request_id and self.preservation_state not in {
            "native_completed", "failed", "pending_recovery",
        }:
            return {"ok": False, "error": "preservation-already-pending", "state": self.state()}
        if not self.enabled:
            return {"ok": False, "error": "protection-disabled", "state": self.state()}
        if (
            expected_session_id != self.session_id
            or expected_supervisor_pid != os.getpid()
            or expected_epoch != self.completed_turn_epoch
        ):
            return {"ok": False, "error": "preservation-owner-mismatch", "state": self.state()}
        self.preservation_request_id = request_id
        self.preservation_reservation_id = reservation_id
        self.preservation_state = "reserved"
        self.preservation_failure = None
        self.preservation_expected_epoch = expected_epoch
        self._save_preservation()
        self._guard_log(
            "preservation-reserved",
            request_id=request_id,
            reservation_id=reservation_id,
        )
        self.preservation_state = "requested"
        self._save_preservation()
        self._guard_log("preservation-requested", request_id=request_id)
        if (
            self.status == "idle"
            and self.transcript_quiescence_deadline is None
            and self.transcript_quiescence_at is not None
        ):
            self.attempt(command="/compact", purpose="compact")
        self._save_preservation()
        self._write_state()
        return {"ok": True, "state": self.state()}

    def _handle_control(self, message: dict) -> dict:
        """Handle one local control command without exposing prompts or secrets."""
        if message.get("kind") != "control":
            return {"ok": False, "error": "invalid-control"}
        if message.get("channel") != self.channel:
            return {"ok": False, "error": "invalid-control-owner"}
        command = message.get("command")
        if command not in CONTROL_COMMANDS:
            return {"ok": False, "error": "unknown-control"}
        if command == "preserve":
            return self._request_preservation(message)
        if command == "state":
            return {"ok": True, "state": self.state()}
        if command == "enable":
            self.enabled = True
            self.arm()
        elif command == "disable":
            self.enabled = False
            self.cancel("session-disabled")
        elif command == "profile":
            profile = message.get("profile")
            if profile not in {"long", "short", "auto"}:
                return {"ok": False, "error": "invalid-profile"}
            self.cache_profile_requested = profile
            self.cache_profile = effective_profile(profile)
            self.cancel("profile-changed")
            if self.recovery_pending:
                self._arm_recovery()
            else:
                self.arm()
        elif command == "cancel":
            self._abort_recovery("manual-cancel")
        elif command == "trigger":
            success = self.attempt()
            self._write_state()
            return {"ok": success, "state": self.state()}
        elif command == "inject":
            text = message.get("text")
            if not validate_inject_command(text, self.watched_servers, self.recovery_command):
                return {"ok": False, "error": "inject-command-refused", "state": self.state()}
            purpose = "compact" if text == "/compact" else "manual"
            success = self.attempt(command=text, purpose=purpose)
            self._write_state()
            return {"ok": success, "state": self.state()}
        self._write_state()
        return {"ok": True, "state": self.state()}

    def _serve_one(self, connection: socket.socket) -> None:
        """Read and answer one hook/control request from the local socket."""
        try:
            connection.settimeout(1.0)
            data = connection.recv(65536)
            if not data:
                return
            message = json.loads(data.split(b"\n", 1)[0].decode("utf-8"))
            if not isinstance(message, dict):
                response = {"ok": False, "error": "invalid-json"}
            elif message.get("kind") == "event":
                response = self._handle_event(message)
            else:
                response = self._handle_control(message)
            connection.sendall((json.dumps(response, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n").encode())
        except (OSError, ValueError, UnicodeError) as error:
            self._debug(f"ipc-error channel={short_id(self.channel)} error={error}")
        finally:
            connection.close()

    def _record_input_observations(
        self,
        observations: list[tuple[str, int, bool]],
        forwarded: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        """Record safe input classifications and cancel once for real human input."""
        if not observations:
            return
        summary: dict[str, int] = {}
        human = False
        total = 0
        for kind, length, is_human in observations:
            summary[kind] = summary.get(kind, 0) + length
            total += length
            human = human or is_human
        pending = (
            self.timer_deadline is not None
            or self.transcript_quiescence_deadline is not None
            or self.recovery_pending
        )
        log_key = json.dumps(sorted(summary.items()), separators=(",", ":"))
        log_now = monotonic()
        should_log = human and pending
        if not should_log and (
            log_key != self.last_terminal_input_log_key
            or log_now - self.last_terminal_input_log_at >= 5.0
        ):
            should_log = True
        if should_log:
            self.last_terminal_input_log_at = log_now
            self.last_terminal_input_log_key = log_key
            self._guard_log(
                "terminal-input",
                bytes=total,
                classifications=summary,
                forwarded=forwarded,
                human_activity=human,
            )
            self._debug(
                f"terminal-input bytes={total} classifications={log_key} "
                f"forwarded={str(forwarded).lower()} human_activity={str(human).lower()}"
            )
        if not human:
            return
        self.mailcortex_input_dirty = True
        if self.hold_timer is not None:
            self.hold_timer.clear()
        if self.dialog_timer is not None:
            self.dialog_timer.clear()
        self._close_development_channel_acceptance("local-input")
        self.last_local_input = now()
        self.last_local_input_classification = ",".join(sorted(summary))
        if not pending:
            self._write_state()
            return
        if self.recovery_pending:
            self._abort_recovery("user-input")
        elif self.last_normal_stop and self.last_local_input > self.last_normal_stop:
            self._defer_for_activity("user-input")
        else:
            self._write_state()

    def _flush_input_classifier(self) -> None:
        """Resolve a lone or incomplete escape after the bounded sequence window."""
        self._record_input_observations(self.input_classifier.flush_expired())

    def _read_stdin(self) -> None:
        with self.mailcortex_admission_lock:
            self._read_stdin_locked()

    def _read_stdin_locked(self) -> None:
        """Proxy every byte while distinguishing terminal protocol from human input."""
        try:
            data = os.read(sys.stdin.fileno(), 65536)
        except (OSError, BlockingIOError):
            return
        if not data:
            self.stdin_eof = True
            if self.master_fd is not None:
                try:
                    write_all(self.master_fd, b"\x04")
                except OSError:
                    pass
            try:
                self.selector.unregister(sys.stdin)
            except Exception:
                pass
            return
        forwarded = False
        if self.master_fd is not None:
            try:
                write_all(self.master_fd, data)
                forwarded = True
            except OSError:
                self.shutdown_requested = True
        try:
            observations = self.input_classifier.feed(data)
        except Exception:
            self._debug("terminal-input-classifier-error")
            observations = [("unknown-input", len(data), True)]
        self._record_input_observations(observations, forwarded=forwarded)
        self._write_state()

    def _read_master(self) -> bool:
        with self.mailcortex_admission_lock:
            return self._read_master_locked()

    def _read_master_locked(self) -> bool:
        """Proxy Claude output to the caller and report whether the PTY remains open."""
        if self.master_fd is None:
            return False
        try:
            data = os.read(self.master_fd, 65536)
        except OSError as error:
            if error.errno in {errno.EIO, errno.EBADF}:
                return False
            return True
        if not data:
            return False
        state = self.screen_classifier.feed(data)
        try:
            write_all(sys.stdout.fileno(), data)
            sys.stdout.flush()
        except OSError:
            self.shutdown_requested = True
        self._maybe_accept_development_channel_prompt(data)
        if state is not None:
            self._report_screen_state(state)
        self._hold_maybe_record(state)
        self._dialog_maybe_record()
        return True

    def _mailcortex_barrier(self):
        """Use the same acknowledged, crash-safe process-tree pause as wake."""
        return _hold_guardian_module().paused_child(self)

    def _hold_input_pending(self):
        return _hold_guardian_module().pending_terminal(self)

    def _mailcortex_refresh_screen(self):
        """Bounded drain under the shared lock; no startup auto-confirm path."""
        import select
        changed = False
        for _ in range(16):
            ready, _, _ = select.select([self.master_fd], [], [], 0)
            if not ready:
                break
            data = os.read(self.master_fd, 65536)
            if not data:
                raise ValueError("child PTY closed")
            changed = True
            self.screen_classifier.feed(data)
            write_all(sys.stdout.fileno(), data)
        else:
            raise ValueError("child output exceeds barrier bound")
        self.screen_state = self.screen_classifier._classify(self.screen_classifier._visible_text())
        return changed

    def _hold_screen(self):
        """An exact permission dialog from the shared attributed screen model."""
        classifier = self.screen_classifier
        visible = classifier._visible_text()
        profile = _hold_profile()
        if (profile is None or not classifier.screen.complete()
                or classifier._classify(visible) != "blocked" or not profile.matches(visible)
                or classifier.prompt_input_state(permission_selection=True) != "empty"):
            return None
        return visible

    def _hold_maybe_record(self, state: str | None) -> None:
        # feed() returns None on an unchanged classification. Re-read the full
        # model so repeated redraws keep the deadline instead of clearing it.
        profile = _hold_profile()
        if profile is None:
            return
        visible = self._hold_screen()
        if visible is not None and not self.mailcortex_input_dirty:
            if self.hold_timer is None:
                self.hold_timer = _hold_module().PtyHoldTimer(timeout=_hold_timeout_seconds())
            if self.hold_timer.record(visible, profile, now=_hold_now()):
                self.hold_started_wall = time.time()
        elif self.hold_timer is not None:
            self.hold_timer.clear()

    def _hold_expire(self) -> None:
        timer = self.hold_timer
        if timer is None or not timer.expired(_hold_now()):
            return
        profile = _hold_profile()
        if profile is None:
            return
        _hold_module().deny_expired_prompt(
            self, timer, profile, read_screen=self._hold_screen, now=_hold_now,
            queue_write=lambda identity: _hold_queue_write(self, profile, identity))

    def _hold_denied(self, queue_id) -> None:
        """A hold-timeout deny landed. The plain owner cannot admit text, so
        it only records the fact; the mail-wake owner explains the refusal."""
        self._guard_log("pty-hold-denied-unexplained")

    def _hold_notice_tick(self) -> None:
        """Loop hook for owners that deliver a pending hold notice."""
        return None

    def _dialog_screen(self):
        """The exact visible screen of a known non-permission blocking dialog,
        only while the seat is otherwise idle on it (never blocked/working)."""
        classifier = self.screen_classifier
        visible = classifier._visible_text()
        if not classifier.screen.complete():
            return None, None
        state = classifier._classify(visible)
        if state not in {"idle", "blocked"}:
            return None, None
        dialog = match_known_dialog(visible)
        if dialog is None or (state == "blocked" and not dialog.allow_blocked):
            return None, None
        return visible, dialog

    def _dialog_visible(self):
        return self._dialog_screen()[0]

    def _dialog_maybe_record(self) -> None:
        # Same re-read discipline as the permission hold: repeated redraws of
        # the same dialog keep the settle deadline instead of clearing it.
        module = _hold_module()
        if module is None:
            return
        visible, dialog = self._dialog_screen()
        if dialog is not None and not self.mailcortex_input_dirty:
            if self.dialog_timer is None:
                self.dialog_timer = module.PtyHoldTimer(timeout=DIALOG_SETTLE_SECONDS)
            self.dialog_timer.record(visible, dialog, now=_hold_now())
        elif self.dialog_timer is not None:
            self.dialog_timer.clear()
        if dialog is None:
            self._dialog_maybe_log_unknown()

    def _dialog_maybe_log_unknown(self) -> None:
        """Record an unidentified blocking dialog once per episode; never key it."""
        classifier = self.screen_classifier
        visible = classifier._visible_text()
        if not classifier.screen.complete():
            return
        legend = dialog_cue_legend(visible)
        if legend is None:
            self.dialog_unknown_logged = False
            return
        if (classifier._classify(visible) != "idle"
                or match_known_dialog(visible) is not None):
            return
        profile = _hold_profile()
        if profile is not None and profile.matches(visible):
            return
        if self.dialog_unknown_logged:
            return
        if _dialog_ledger_write(self, "dialog_unknown",
                                snippet=" ".join(visible[-400:].split())):
            self.dialog_unknown_logged = True

    def _dialog_expire(self) -> None:
        timer = self.dialog_timer
        if timer is None:
            return
        _visible, dialog = self._dialog_screen()
        if dialog is None:
            timer.clear()
            return
        dismiss_expired_dialog(
            self, timer, dialog, read_screen=self._dialog_visible, now=_hold_now,
            ledger_write=lambda identity: _dialog_ledger_write(
                self, "dialog_dismissed", dialog.dialog_id, identity))

    def _child_has_exited(self) -> bool:
        """Reap the child once and retain its real exit status."""
        if self.child_status is not None or not self.pid:
            return self.child_status is not None
        try:
            waited_pid, status = os.waitpid(self.pid, os.WNOHANG)
        except ChildProcessError:
            self.child_status = 1 << 8
            return True
        if waited_pid == self.pid:
            self.child_status = status
            self.child_exited_at = now()
            self.status = "exited"
            self.cancel("process-exit")
            self._guard_log("process-exit", exit_code=os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status))
            return True
        return False

    def _handle_inotify(self) -> None:
        """Classify complete transcript records after a watcher notification."""
        if not self.inotify:
            return
        for mask, name in self.inotify.read():
            if mask & Inotify.IN_Q_OVERFLOW:
                self._record_transcript_observation(
                    "watcher-overflow", gate_blocked=True
                )
                self._record_transcript_activity()
            elif self.transcript_path and name == self.transcript_path.name:
                self._record_transcript_activity()

    def run(self) -> int:
        """Run the PTY proxy until Claude exits, then restore and clean up."""
        self.launch()
        master_open = True
        try:
            while master_open:
                child_exited = self._child_has_exited()
                self.herdr.heartbeat(self.screen_state, self.session_id)
                self.preservation_federation.poll()
                self._check_channel_attach()
                self._expire_development_channel_acceptance()
                if not child_exited:
                    self._check_lifecycle_hooks()
                self._flush_input_classifier()
                if (
                    self.transcript_quiescence_deadline is not None
                    and now() >= self.transcript_quiescence_deadline
                ):
                    self._complete_transcript_quiescence()
                # Universal PTY hold-with-timeout: fire the guarded deny when
                # the recorded permission prompt outlives the deadline. The
                # deny itself re-validates everything under the owner lock.
                if self.hold_timer is not None and self.hold_timer.expired(_hold_now()):
                    self._hold_expire()
                self._hold_notice_tick()
                # Known non-permission dialogs (autocompact prompt): dismiss
                # with the entry's safe key after the short settle. Unknown
                # dialogs are only logged, never keyed.
                if self.dialog_timer is not None and self.dialog_timer.expired(_hold_now()):
                    self._dialog_expire()
                if (
                    self.compacting
                    and self.compaction_requested_at
                    and now() - self.compaction_requested_at >= COMPACT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
                ):
                    # No PostCompact and no rejection marker inside the
                    # timeout: the injected command was lost (or is queued
                    # behind a turn that has not ended).  Clear the latch so
                    # the governor can try again instead of sitting in
                    # `compacting' forever.  Unlike Claude's explicit
                    # rejection this does not swallow the next Stop: that
                    # Stop is the end of a real turn and must arm normally.
                    self.compacting = False
                    self.compaction_requested_at = None
                    self.compaction_ignore_next_stop = False
                    self.dirty = True
                    self.last_cancellation_reason = "compaction-timeout"
                    self.last_injection_result = "failed"
                    self.last_state_transition = "compaction-timeout"
                    if self.preservation_state == "native_started":
                        self.preservation_state = "failed"
                        self.preservation_failure = "compaction-timeout"
                        self._save_preservation()
                        self._guard_log(
                            "preservation-failed", request_id=self.preservation_request_id, reason="timeout"
                        )
                    self._debug(f"compaction-timeout channel={short_id(self.channel)}")
                    self._guard_log("compaction-failed", reason="timeout")
                    self._release_after_failed_compaction("timeout")
                    self.rearm("compaction-timeout")
                    self._write_state()
                if self.timer_deadline is not None and now() >= self.timer_deadline:
                    generation = self.timer_generation
                    kind = self.timer_kind
                    self.timer_deadline = None
                    self.timer_kind = None
                    if kind == "compact":
                        self.attempt(generation)
                    elif kind == "channel-recovery":
                        self._recovery_step(generation)
                    elif kind == "channel-recovery-confirm":
                        self._recovery_confirmation(generation)
                # inotify is the fast path; this low-frequency reconciliation
                # poll also covers editor-style atomic replacement and dropped
                # queue notifications without becoming a busy scanner.
                if monotonic() >= self.next_transcript_poll:
                    self.next_transcript_poll = monotonic() + TRANSCRIPT_POLL_SECONDS
                    self._record_transcript_activity()
                if monotonic() >= self.next_debug_bound:
                    self.next_debug_bound = monotonic() + 5
                    self._bound_debug_log()
                if child_exited:
                    master_open = self._read_master()
                    if not master_open:
                        break
                timeout = 1.0
                if self.timer_deadline is not None:
                    timeout = max(0.0, min(timeout, self.timer_deadline - now()))
                if self.transcript_quiescence_deadline is not None:
                    timeout = max(
                        0.0,
                        min(timeout, self.transcript_quiescence_deadline - now()),
                    )
                if self.development_channel_accept_deadline is not None:
                    timeout = max(
                        0.0,
                        min(
                            timeout,
                            self.development_channel_accept_deadline - monotonic(),
                        ),
                    )
                timeout = min(timeout, max(0.0, self.next_transcript_poll - monotonic()))
                next_preservation_poll = self.preservation_federation.next_poll
                if next_preservation_poll is not None:
                    timeout = min(timeout, max(0.0, next_preservation_poll - monotonic()))
                if self.preservation_federation.bridge_thread is not None:
                    # Bounded wake strategy for result reaping.  The owner
                    # loop still sleeps while the worker is in flight, and
                    # all state/control mutations happen after reaping here.
                    timeout = min(timeout, PRESERVATION_IN_FLIGHT_WAKE_SECONDS)
                self._ensure_control_socket()
                events = self.selector.select(timeout)
                for key, _mask in events:
                    if key.data == "server":
                        try:
                            connection, _ = self.socket.accept()
                            self._serve_one(connection)
                        except BlockingIOError:
                            pass
                    elif key.data == "master":
                        master_open = self._read_master()
                    elif key.data == "stdin":
                        self._read_stdin()
                    elif key.data == "inotify":
                        self._handle_inotify()
                if self.shutdown_requested and self.pid and child_alive(self.pid):
                    self._forward_signal(signal.SIGHUP, None)
            self._child_has_exited()
            if self.child_status is None and self.pid:
                try:
                    _, self.child_status = os.waitpid(self.pid, 0)
                except ChildProcessError:
                    self.child_status = 1 << 8
            return os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(self.child_status or 0)
        finally:
            self._cleanup_terminal_modes()
            self._restore_terminal()
            self.cleanup()

    def cleanup(self) -> None:
        """Close PTY/IPC resources and remove only this session's runtime directory."""
        self.herdr.release()
        try:
            self.selector.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
        if self.inotify:
            self.inotify.close()
            self.inotify = None
        if self.master_fd is not None:
            try:
                os.close(self.master_fd)
            except OSError:
                pass
            self.master_fd = None
        try:
            self.socket.close()
        except OSError:
            pass
        self._record_last_session("supervisor-exit")
        try:
            shutil.rmtree(self.session_dir)
        except OSError:
            pass


def control_socket(channel: str) -> pathlib.Path:
    """Return a validated session control socket path."""
    if not CHANNEL_RE.fullmatch(channel):
        raise RuntimeError("invalid Claude idle-compaction channel")
    return runtime_root() / channel / "control.sock"


def send_control(channel: str, command: str, **fields: object) -> dict:
    """Send one safe control command to a supervised session."""
    path = control_socket(channel)
    message = {"kind": "control", "channel": channel, "command": command, **fields}
    connection = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    try:
        connection.settimeout(2.0)
        connection.connect(str(path))
        connection.sendall((json.dumps(message, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n").encode())
        data = connection.recv(65536)
        return json.loads(data.decode("utf-8"))
    finally:
        connection.close()


def list_sessions() -> int:
    """List live or stale supervised session state without prompt contents."""
    root = runtime_root()
    found = False
    for state_path in sorted(root.glob("claude-*/state.json")):
        found = True
        try:
            state = json.loads(state_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            continue
        state["live"] = child_alive(state.get("pid"))
        print(json.dumps(state, sort_keys=True))
        if state["live"] and state.get("lifecycle_health") == "missing":
            print(f"WARNING {state.get('channel')}: no lifecycle hook event since launch; "
                  "claude-hook-idle-event is not wired, idle compaction cannot run", file=sys.stderr)
    if not found:
        print("no supervised Claude sessions")
    return 0


def control_main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    """Implement claude-warmctl controls."""
    if not argv or argv[0] in {"-h", "--help"}:
        print("usage: claude-warmctl list | show CHANNEL | enable CHANNEL | disable CHANNEL")
        print("       claude-warmctl profile CHANNEL long|short|auto")
        print("       claude-warmctl cancel CHANNEL | trigger CHANNEL")
        print("       claude-warmctl inject CHANNEL /compact|/reload-plugins|/mcp reconnect SERVER")
        print("       claude-warmctl preserve CHANNEL REQUEST RESERVATION SESSION PID EPOCH")
        return 0
    command = argv[0]
    if command == "list":
        return list_sessions()
    if command not in CONTROL_COMMANDS:
        print(f"unknown control command: {command}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    if len(argv) < 2:
        print(f"{command} requires CHANNEL", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    channel = argv[1]
    fields = {"profile": argv[2]} if command == "profile" and len(argv) >= 3 else {}
    if command == "profile" and not fields:
        print("profile requires long, short, or auto", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    if command == "inject":
        if len(argv) < 3:
            print("inject requires a whitelisted command", file=sys.stderr)
            return 2
        fields = {"text": " ".join(argv[2:])}
    if command == "preserve":
        if len(argv) != 7:
            print("preserve requires CHANNEL REQUEST RESERVATION SESSION PID EPOCH", file=sys.stderr)
            return 2
        try:
            expected_supervisor_pid = int(argv[5])
            expected_epoch = int(argv[6])
        except ValueError:
            print("preserve PID and EPOCH must be integers", file=sys.stderr)
            return 2
        fields = {
            "request_id": argv[2], "reservation_id": argv[3],
            "expected_session_id": argv[4],
            "expected_supervisor_pid": expected_supervisor_pid,
            "expected_epoch": expected_epoch,
        }
    try:
        response = send_control(channel, "state" if command == "show" else command, **fields)
    except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as error:
        print(f"control failed: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    print(json.dumps(response, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
    return 0 if response.get("ok") else 1


def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    """Launch Claude or dispatch a control command."""
    if argv and argv[0] == "--control":
        return control_main(argv[1:])
    if argv and argv[0] in {"-h", "--help"}:
        print("usage: claude-warm [CLAUDE OPTIONS...]", file=sys.stdout)
        print("Runs the real Claude CLI through a full PTY supervisor.")
        print("Environment: CLAUDE_IDLE_CACHE_PROFILE, CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACTION_DEBUG,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_SECONDS, CLAUDE_SHORT_CACHE_IDLE_COMPACT_SECONDS,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_MIN_TOKENS, CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_STATUS_STOP_SKEW_SECONDS,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_RETRY_SECONDS, CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_TRANSCRIPT_QUIESCENCE_SECONDS,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_INPUT_ESCAPE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_WATCHED_SERVERS, CLAUDE_IDLE_RECONNECT_SERVER,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_CHANNEL_ATTACH_WAIT_SECONDS, CLAUDE_IDLE_CHANNEL_RECOVERY_COOLDOWN_SECONDS")
        print("CLAUDE_WARM_ACCEPT_DEV_CHANNELS,")
        print("CLAUDE_WARM_DEV_CHANNEL_ACCEPT_WINDOW_SECONDS")
        print("CLAUDE_WARM_HERDR_AGENT, CLAUDE_WARM_HERDR_HEARTBEAT_SECONDS,")
        print("CLAUDE_WARM_HERDR_REGISTRATION_TTL_MS")
        print("CLAUDE_WARM_PRESERVATION_* (all bindings are required; unset is inert)")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_MODE=native|deterministic (deterministic runs a local,")
        print("provider-free pass over a transcript copy instead of injecting /compact)")
        print("  deterministic is due at the COLD CACHE BOUNDARY (idle + cache at/over its")
        print("  compact-before window) or when the provider is usage-limited -- never")
        print("  merely because the transcript is large; MIN_TOKENS is only a floor)")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_CEILING_TOKENS (total cold reload), CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_CHARS_PER_TOKEN,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_KEEP_RECENT_RECORDS, CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_STUB_OVER_CHARS,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_GROUP_POINTERS=1|0 (group elisions by task into one")
        print("  summarised stub that points at a group in the index; only when it saves),")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_GROUP_MIN_SAVING_CHARS, CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_PROJECT,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_FOLD_PROSE=1|0 (older prose and tool arguments leave the")
        print("  working copy as indexed pointers; the recent tail stays verbatim),")
        print("CLAUDE_PROJECTS_DIR, CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_STATE_DIR, CLAUDE_WARM_OFFLINE_COMPACT_MODULE")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_AGENT_FAMILY=claude|codex, CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_SCOPE=helmcortex|dotcortex,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_HARNESS_TOKENS (override), CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_HARNESS_INDEX_TOKENS")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_DECIDER=auto|off, CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_HONEY_DECIDER_URL,")
        print("CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_KIKIN_DECIDER_URL, CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_DECIDER_BUDGET_MS")
        print("Use claude-warmctl for per-session state and controls.")
        return 0
    try:
        supervisor = Supervisor(argv, resolve_claude())
        return supervisor.run()
    except (OSError, RuntimeError) as error:
        print(f"claude-warm: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 69


def shell_exit_code(status: int) -> int:
    """Represent a child signal termination with the conventional 128+N code."""
    return 128 - status if status < 0 else status


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(shell_exit_code(main(sys.argv[1:])))
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/bin/claude-warmctl :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env bash" :comments no
set -euo pipefail
exec "$(dirname "$0")/claude-warm" --control "$@"
#+END_SRC

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/bin/claude-warm-live-observe :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env python3" :comments no
"""Observe one accelerated Claude idle-compaction acceptance run.

This command is deliberately read-only.  It discovers a newly-created
supervised Claude session, reads its state, status snapshot, and safe guard
log, and reports the real lifecycle.  The Claude PTY is never opened and the
supervisor control socket is never contacted.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as dt
import json
import math
import os
import pathlib
import re
import shutil
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from typing import Any


CHANNEL_RE = re.compile(r"^claude-[0-9a-f]{32}$")
NUMERIC_TIMESTAMP_RE = re.compile(r"^[+-]?(?:\d+(?:\.\d*)?|\.\d+)(?:[eE][+-]?\d+)?$")
FORMER_SNAPSHOT_MAX_AGE_SECONDS = 120.0


def runtime_root() -> pathlib.Path:
    value = os.environ.get("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR")
    if not value:
        raise RuntimeError("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR is not set")
    return pathlib.Path(value) / "claude-idle-compaction"


def control_command() -> pathlib.Path:
    sibling = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "claude-warmctl"
    if sibling.is_file():
        return sibling
    found = shutil.which("claude-warmctl")
    if found:
        return pathlib.Path(found)
    raise RuntimeError("claude-warmctl is not available beside the observer")


def parse_records(text: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Accept the installed JSONL list shape and a future JSON array shape."""
    stripped = text.strip()
    if not stripped or stripped == "no supervised Claude sessions":
        return []
    try:
        value = json.loads(stripped)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        value = None
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return [value] if isinstance(value.get("channel"), str) else []
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [item for item in value if isinstance(item, dict)]
    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for line in text.splitlines():
        try:
            item = json.loads(line)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue
        if isinstance(item, dict) and isinstance(item.get("channel"), str):
            records.append(item)
    return records


def list_sessions(command: pathlib.Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    result = subprocess.run(
        [str(command), "list"],
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        timeout=5,
    )
    return parse_records(result.stdout)


def valid_channel(value: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, str) and CHANNEL_RE.fullmatch(value) is not None


def read_state(root: pathlib.Path, channel: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    if not valid_channel(channel):
        return None
    path = root / channel / "state.json"
    try:
        value = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return None
    return value if isinstance(value, dict) else None


def process_present(value: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool) and pathlib.Path(f"/proc/{value}").exists()


def supervisor_environment(pid: Any) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Read only the five safe accelerated-test settings from the owner PID."""
    if not process_present(pid):
        return {}
    wanted = {
        "CLAUDE_IDLE_CACHE_PROFILE",
        "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_SECONDS",
        "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_LONG_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS",
        "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_MIN_TOKENS",
        "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACTION_DEBUG",
    }
    try:
        entries = pathlib.Path(f"/proc/{pid}/environ").read_bytes().split(b"\0")
    except OSError:
        return {}
    values: dict[str, str] = {}
    for entry in entries:
        key, separator, value = entry.partition(b"=")
        if separator and key.decode("utf-8", "replace") in wanted:
            values[key.decode("utf-8", "replace")] = value.decode("utf-8", "replace")
    return values


def timestamp_epoch(value: Any) -> tuple[float, str] | None:
    """Parse the status wrapper's numeric or ISO timestamp without shell arithmetic."""
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return None
    if isinstance(value, int):
        number = float(value)
        kind = "integer"
    elif isinstance(value, float):
        number = value
        kind = "float"
    elif isinstance(value, str):
        text = value.strip()
        if NUMERIC_TIMESTAMP_RE.fullmatch(text):
            try:
                number = float(text)
            except ValueError:
                return None
            kind = "numeric-string"
        else:
            try:
                parsed = dt.datetime.fromisoformat(text.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
            except ValueError:
                return None
            if parsed.tzinfo is None:
                parsed = parsed.replace(tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc)
            number = parsed.timestamp()
            kind = "iso8601"
    else:
        return None
    if not math.isfinite(number):
        return None
    return number, kind


def read_status(root: pathlib.Path, state: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], float, str, int] | None:
    channel = state.get("channel")
    session_id = state.get("session_id")
    if not valid_channel(channel) or not isinstance(session_id, str) or not session_id:
        return None
    path = root / channel / "status.json"
    try:
        value = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return None
    if not isinstance(value, dict):
        return None
    if value.get("channel") != channel or value.get("session_id") != session_id:
        return None
    tokens = value.get("current_input_context_tokens")
    if not isinstance(tokens, int) or isinstance(tokens, bool) or tokens < 0:
        return None
    timestamp = timestamp_epoch(value.get("update_timestamp"))
    if timestamp is None:
        return None
    return value, timestamp[0], timestamp[1], tokens


def read_guard(path: pathlib.Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    try:
        lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    except OSError:
        return []
    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for line in lines:
        try:
            value = json.loads(line)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue
        if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(value.get("event"), str):
            records.append(value)
    return records


def event_count(events: list[dict[str, Any]], name: str, purpose: str | None = None) -> int:
    return sum(
        1
        for event in events
        if event.get("event") == name and (purpose is None or event.get("purpose") == purpose)
    )


def event_timestamp(event: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    parsed = timestamp_epoch(event.get("timestamp"))
    return parsed[0] if parsed else None


def sleep_poll(deadline: float, interval: float = 0.5) -> None:
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 0:
        time.sleep(min(interval, remaining))


def state_summary(state: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not state:
        return {}
    return {
        "channel": state.get("channel"),
        "supervisor_pid": state.get("supervisor_pid"),
        "claude_pid": state.get("pid"),
        "session_id": state.get("session_id"),
        "cache_profile": state.get("cache_profile"),
        "current_input_context_tokens": state.get("current_input_context_tokens"),
        "dirty": state.get("dirty"),
        "compacting": state.get("compacting"),
        "timer_deadline": state.get("timer_deadline"),
        "timer_kind": state.get("timer_kind"),
        "last_injection_result": state.get("last_injection_result"),
        "last_cancellation_reason": state.get("last_cancellation_reason"),
        "last_gate_rejection_reason": state.get("last_gate_rejection_reason"),
    }


def print_summary(summary: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    print("claude-warm-live-observe summary")
    for key, value in summary.items():
        print(f"{key}={json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True)}")


def run(arguments: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    root = runtime_root()
    command = control_command()
    baseline = {record.get("channel") for record in list_sessions(command) if valid_channel(record.get("channel"))}
    print("observer-ready: launch the one disposable accelerated session now")
    print("observer-action=read-only-state-and-log-polling")
    sys.stdout.flush()

    deadline = time.monotonic() + arguments.discovery_timeout
    channel: str | None = None
    state: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        for record in list_sessions(command):
            candidate = record.get("channel")
            if not valid_channel(candidate) or candidate in baseline:
                continue
            fresh = read_state(root, candidate)
            if not fresh or not process_present(fresh.get("supervisor_pid")) or not process_present(fresh.get("pid")):
                continue
            channel = candidate
            state = fresh
            break
        if channel:
            break
        sleep_poll(deadline)
    if not channel or not state:
        print_summary({"result": "FAIL", "failure_reason": "new-live-supervised-session-not-discovered"})
        return 1

    expected_environment = {
        "CLAUDE_IDLE_CACHE_PROFILE": "long",
        "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_SECONDS": str(int(arguments.expected_delay)),
        "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_LONG_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS": str(int(arguments.expected_ttl)),
        "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACT_MIN_TOKENS": str(arguments.expected_threshold),
        "CLAUDE_IDLE_COMPACTION_DEBUG": "1",
    }
    configured_environment = supervisor_environment(state.get("supervisor_pid"))
    if configured_environment != expected_environment:
        print_summary(
            {
                "result": "FAIL",
                "failure_reason": "accelerated-supervisor-environment-mismatch",
                "expected_environment": expected_environment,
                "configured_environment": configured_environment,
                **state_summary(state),
            }
        )
        return 1

    guard_path_value = state.get("channel_guard_log")
    if not isinstance(guard_path_value, str):
        print_summary({"result": "FAIL", "failure_reason": "guard-log-path-missing", **state_summary(state)})
        return 1
    guard_path = pathlib.Path(guard_path_value)
    last_status: tuple[float, str, int] | None = None
    failure_reason: str | None = None
    snapshot_age: float | None = None
    compact_request: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    armed_deadline: float | None = None
    arm_deadline = time.monotonic() + arguments.arm_timeout

    def observe() -> tuple[dict[str, Any], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
        nonlocal state, last_status
        current = read_state(root, channel) or {}
        status = read_status(root, current)
        if status is not None:
            _, timestamp, kind, tokens = status
            last_status = (timestamp, kind, tokens)
        state = current
        return current, read_guard(guard_path)

    while time.monotonic() < arm_deadline:
        state, events = observe()
        arms = [event for event in events if event.get("event") == "armed" and event.get("kind") == "compact"]
        failures = [
            event
            for event in events
            if event.get("event") == "gate-refused" and event.get("purpose") == "compact"
        ]
        if len(arms) > 1:
            failure_reason = "duplicate-compact-timers"
            break
        if failures:
            failure_reason = f"gate-refused:{failures[-1].get('reason', 'unknown')}"
            break
        if arms and state.get("timer_kind") == "compact" and state.get("timer_deadline") is not None:
            if armed_deadline is None:
                try:
                    armed_deadline = float(state["timer_deadline"])
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    failure_reason = "invalid-compact-timer-deadline"
                    break
            armed_delay = arms[-1].get("delay")
            try:
                delay_matches = abs(float(armed_delay) - arguments.expected_delay) < 0.1
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                delay_matches = False
            if not delay_matches:
                failure_reason = "unexpected-compact-delay"
                break
            if state.get("cache_profile") != "long":
                failure_reason = "unexpected-cache-profile"
                break
            if last_status is not None and last_status[2] >= arguments.expected_threshold:
                break
        if not process_present(state.get("supervisor_pid")) or not process_present(state.get("pid")):
            failure_reason = "supervisor-or-claude-exited-before-arm"
            break
        sleep_poll(arm_deadline)
    else:
        failure_reason = "compact-timer-not-armed-before-timeout"

    if failure_reason is None:
        compact_deadline = time.monotonic() + arguments.compaction_timeout
        while time.monotonic() < compact_deadline:
            state, events = observe()
            arms = [event for event in events if event.get("event") == "armed" and event.get("kind") == "compact"]
            requests = [
                event
                for event in events
                if event.get("event") == "compaction-requested"
            ]
            attempts = [
                event
                for event in events
                if event.get("event") == "injection-attempted" and event.get("purpose") == "compact"
            ]
            stale = [
                event
                for event in events
                if event.get("reason") == "status-snapshot-stale"
            ]
            if len(arms) > 1:
                failure_reason = "duplicate-compact-timers"
                break
            if stale:
                failure_reason = "status-snapshot-stale-rejection"
                break
            gate_failures = [
                event
                for event in events
                if event.get("event") == "gate-refused" and event.get("purpose") == "compact"
            ]
            if gate_failures:
                failure_reason = f"gate-refused:{gate_failures[-1].get('reason', 'unknown')}"
                break
            if state.get("last_state_transition") == "injection-failed" or state.get("last_injection_result") == "failed":
                failure_reason = "compaction-injection-failed"
                break
            if len(requests) > 1 or len(attempts) > 1:
                failure_reason = "duplicate-compaction-injection"
                break
            if requests and compact_request is None:
                compact_request = requests[0]
                if last_status is None:
                    failure_reason = "no-valid-status-snapshot-at-callback"
                    break
                request_time = event_timestamp(compact_request)
                if request_time is None:
                    failure_reason = "invalid-compaction-request-timestamp"
                    break
                snapshot_age = request_time - last_status[0]
                if snapshot_age <= arguments.former_snapshot_max_age:
                    failure_reason = "snapshot-was-not-older-than-regression-limit"
                    break
            completed = [event for event in events if event.get("event") == "compaction-completed"]
            if completed:
                if len(completed) != 1:
                    failure_reason = "duplicate-compaction-completions"
                    break
                break
            if not process_present(state.get("supervisor_pid")) or not process_present(state.get("pid")):
                failure_reason = "supervisor-or-claude-exited-before-compaction-completed"
                break
            sleep_poll(compact_deadline)
        else:
            failure_reason = "compaction-completion-timeout"

    quiet_deadline = time.monotonic() + arguments.quiet_seconds if failure_reason is None else time.monotonic()
    while failure_reason is None and time.monotonic() < quiet_deadline:
        state, events = observe()
        arms = [event for event in events if event.get("event") == "armed" and event.get("kind") == "compact"]
        if len(arms) != 1:
            failure_reason = "idle-compaction-rearmed-after-completion"
            break
        sleep_poll(quiet_deadline)

    state = read_state(root, channel) or state
    events = read_guard(guard_path)
    arms = [event for event in events if event.get("event") == "armed" and event.get("kind") == "compact"]
    attempts = [
        event
        for event in events
        if event.get("event") == "injection-attempted" and event.get("purpose") == "compact"
    ]
    requests = [event for event in events if event.get("event") == "compaction-requested"]
    completed = [event for event in events if event.get("event") == "compaction-completed"]
    stale = [event for event in events if event.get("reason") == "status-snapshot-stale"]
    away_summary_events = [
        event
        for event in events
        if event.get("event") == "transcript-record-ignored"
        and event.get("record_type") == "system"
        and event.get("record_subtype") == "away_summary"
    ]
    transcript_cancellations = [
        event
        for event in events
        if event.get("event") == "cancelled"
        and event.get("reason") == "transcript-activity"
    ]
    away_summary_timer_preserved = bool(
        away_summary_events
        and armed_deadline is not None
        and not transcript_cancellations
    )
    terminal_untouched = True
    success = (
        failure_reason is None
        and len(arms) == 1
        and len(attempts) == 1
        and len(requests) == 1
        and len(completed) == 1
        and not stale
        and state.get("dirty") is False
        and state.get("compacting") is False
        and state.get("timer_deadline") is None
        and state.get("timer_kind") is None
        and state.get("last_injection_result") == "succeeded"
        and away_summary_timer_preserved
        and process_present(state.get("supervisor_pid"))
        and process_present(state.get("pid"))
    )
    if not success and failure_reason is None:
        failure_reason = "final-state-assertion-failed"
    summary = {
        "result": "PASS" if success else "FAIL",
        "failure_reason": failure_reason,
        "channel": channel,
        "session_id": state.get("session_id"),
        "supervisor_pid": state.get("supervisor_pid"),
        "claude_pid": state.get("pid"),
        "status_snapshot_type": last_status[1] if last_status else None,
        "snapshot_age_at_callback_seconds": round(snapshot_age, 3) if snapshot_age is not None else None,
        "original_timer_deadline": armed_deadline,
        "away_summary_observed": len(away_summary_events),
        "away_summary_classification": (
            away_summary_events[-1].get("classification") if away_summary_events else None
        ),
        "away_summary_timer_preserved": away_summary_timer_preserved,
        "compact_timers_armed": len(arms),
        "injection_attempts": len(attempts),
        "compaction_requests": len(requests),
        "completed_compactions": len(completed),
        "stale_snapshot_rejections": len(stale),
        "final_dirty": state.get("dirty"),
        "final_compacting": state.get("compacting"),
        "final_timer_deadline": state.get("timer_deadline"),
        "final_timer_kind": state.get("timer_kind"),
        "final_injection_result": state.get("last_injection_result"),
        "last_cancellation_reason": state.get("last_cancellation_reason"),
        "last_gate_rejection_reason": state.get("last_gate_rejection_reason"),
        "terminal_manipulation": "none",
        "supervisor_live_at_observation_end": process_present(state.get("supervisor_pid")),
        "claude_live_at_observation_end": process_present(state.get("pid")),
        "expected_delay_seconds": arguments.expected_delay,
        "expected_presumed_ttl_seconds": arguments.expected_ttl,
        "expected_token_threshold": arguments.expected_threshold,
        "accelerated_supervisor_environment_match": configured_environment == expected_environment,
    }
    print_summary(summary)
    return 0 if success else 1


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    """Build the observer CLI parser, including the regression-report bound."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--discovery-timeout", type=float, default=1200.0)
    parser.add_argument("--arm-timeout", type=float, default=180.0)
    parser.add_argument("--compaction-timeout", type=float, default=420.0)
    parser.add_argument("--quiet-seconds", type=float, default=12.0)
    parser.add_argument("--expected-delay", type=float, default=140.0)
    parser.add_argument("--expected-ttl", type=float, default=300.0)
    parser.add_argument("--expected-threshold", type=int, default=1)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--former-snapshot-max-age",
        type=float,
        default=FORMER_SNAPSHOT_MAX_AGE_SECONDS,
        help="Require the callback snapshot to be older than this regression bound.",
    )
    return parser


def main() -> int:
    parser = build_parser()
    try:
        return run(parser.parse_args())
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print_summary({"result": "FAIL", "failure_reason": "observer-interrupted", "terminal_manipulation": "none"})
        return 130
    except (OSError, RuntimeError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
        print_summary({"result": "FAIL", "failure_reason": f"observer-error:{error}", "terminal_manipulation": "none"})
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
#+END_SRC

* Telegram poller liveness watchdog

The warm supervisor can remain alive after its Telegram long-poller has lost
update ownership.  Process supervision alone cannot see that state.  This
watchdog reconciles each labelled warm-session record with its live runtime
state and the plugin's =bot.pid= lock.  It deliberately ignores poller-log
mtime because a healthy idle long-poller may write nothing for hours.

Auto-revive is fail-closed.  The current supervisor and Claude child must both
be alive and ancestrally coherent, the runtime state must identify the same
session and report =idle=, and the poller must be missing, dead, or outside the
supervisor's process tree.  Missing state, clean exits, active turns,
compaction, and the startup grace window are skips.  Revives use Nurse Joy's
own home-machine and graceful-exit guards, never =--force=, with a persistent
cooldown and an extended alert cooldown after repeated failures.  A deaf
observation must also persist across watchdog ticks before any action, so a
brief plugin restart cannot revive an otherwise healthy session.

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/bin/claude-poller-watchdog :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env python3" :comments no
"""Detect deaf Claude Telegram pollers and request a guarded Nurse Joy revive."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import fcntl
import json
import os
import pathlib
import re
import shutil
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time


SCHEMA = "dotcortex.claude-poller-watchdog.v1"
LABEL_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}$")
SESSION_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,240}$")
CHANNEL_RE = re.compile(r"^claude-[0-9a-f]{32}$")
DEFAULT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 30 * 60
DEFAULT_ALERT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 6 * 60 * 60
DEFAULT_STARTUP_GRACE_SECONDS = 5 * 60
DEFAULT_CONFIRM_SECONDS = 2 * 60
DEFAULT_MAX_FAILURES = 3


def read_json(path: pathlib.Path) -> dict | None:
    try:
        payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None
    return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else None


def atomic_json(path: pathlib.Path, payload: dict) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            json.dump(payload, stream, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
            stream.write("\n")
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        os.chmod(temporary, 0o600)
        os.replace(temporary, path)
    finally:
        try:
            os.unlink(temporary)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass


def proc_identity(pid: object, proc_root: pathlib.Path) -> tuple[int, str, int] | None:
    if not isinstance(pid, int) or pid <= 1:
        return None
    try:
        text = (proc_root / str(pid) / "stat").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        tail = text[text.rfind(")") + 2 :].split()
        state = tail[0]
        parent = int(tail[1])
        start_ticks = int(tail[19])
    except (OSError, ValueError, IndexError):
        return None
    return (parent, state, start_ticks)


def process_alive(pid: object, proc_root: pathlib.Path) -> bool:
    identity = proc_identity(pid, proc_root)
    if identity is None or identity[1] == "Z":
        return False
    if proc_root == pathlib.Path("/proc"):
        try:
            os.kill(int(pid), 0)
        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
            return False
    return True


def process_fingerprint(pid: int, proc_root: pathlib.Path) -> str | None:
    identity = proc_identity(pid, proc_root)
    return f"{pid}:{identity[2]}" if identity is not None else None


def is_descendant(pid: int, ancestor: int, proc_root: pathlib.Path) -> bool:
    if pid == ancestor:
        return True
    seen: set[int] = set()
    current = pid
    for _ in range(128):
        if current in seen or current <= 1:
            return False
        seen.add(current)
        identity = proc_identity(current, proc_root)
        if identity is None:
            return False
        current = identity[0]
        if current == ancestor:
            return True
    return False


def telegram_enabled(argv: object) -> bool:
    if not isinstance(argv, list):
        return False
    for index, item in enumerate(argv[:-1]):
        if item == "--channels" and "telegram" in str(argv[index + 1]).lower():
            return True
    return False


def poller_pid(path: pathlib.Path) -> int | None:
    try:
        value = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    except OSError:
        return None
    if not value.isdigit():
        return None
    parsed = int(value)
    return parsed if parsed > 1 else None


def result(label: str, status: str, reason: str, **fields: object) -> dict:
    return {"schema": SCHEMA, "label": label, "status": status, "reason": reason, **fields}


def assess_record(
    record: dict,
    *,
    state_root: pathlib.Path,
    runtime_root: pathlib.Path,
    channels_root: pathlib.Path,
    proc_root: pathlib.Path,
    current_time: float,
    startup_grace: float,
) -> dict:
    label = record.get("agent")
    if not isinstance(label, str) or not LABEL_RE.fullmatch(label):
        return result("unknown", "skip", "invalid-label")
    if not telegram_enabled(record.get("argv")):
        return result(label, "skip", "telegram-not-enabled")
    if record.get("event") == "supervisor-exit":
        return result(label, "skip", "deliberate-exit")
    session_id = record.get("session_id")
    channel = record.get("channel")
    if not isinstance(session_id, str) or not SESSION_RE.fullmatch(session_id):
        return result(label, "skip", "invalid-session")
    if not isinstance(channel, str) or not CHANNEL_RE.fullmatch(channel):
        return result(label, "skip", "invalid-channel")

    runtime = read_json(runtime_root / channel / "state.json")
    if runtime is None:
        return result(label, "skip", "missing-runtime-state")
    expected_supervisor = record.get("supervisor_pid")
    expected_child = record.get("pid")
    if (
        runtime.get("session_id") != session_id
        or runtime.get("supervisor_pid") != expected_supervisor
        or runtime.get("pid") != expected_child
    ):
        return result(label, "skip", "runtime-record-mismatch")
    if runtime.get("status") == "exited":
        return result(label, "skip", "deliberate-exit")
    if not process_alive(expected_supervisor, proc_root) or not process_alive(expected_child, proc_root):
        return result(label, "skip", "owner-not-live")
    if not is_descendant(expected_child, expected_supervisor, proc_root):
        return result(label, "skip", "owner-tree-incoherent")
    owner_fingerprint = (
        f"{process_fingerprint(expected_supervisor, proc_root)}/"
        f"{process_fingerprint(expected_child, proc_root)}"
    )

    recorded_at = record.get("recorded_at")
    if not isinstance(recorded_at, (int, float)) or current_time - float(recorded_at) < startup_grace:
        return result(label, "skip", "startup-grace")
    if runtime.get("compacting"):
        return result(label, "skip", "compacting")
    if runtime.get("status") != "idle":
        return result(label, "skip", "owner-busy", owner_status=runtime.get("status"))

    lock = channels_root / f"telegram-{label.lower()}" / "bot.pid"
    pid = poller_pid(lock)
    if pid is None:
        return result(label, "deaf", "poller-lock-missing-or-invalid", _fingerprint=f"{owner_fingerprint}/none")
    if not process_alive(pid, proc_root):
        return result(
            label, "deaf", "poller-dead", poller_pid=pid,
            _fingerprint=f"{owner_fingerprint}/{process_fingerprint(pid, proc_root)}",
        )
    if not is_descendant(pid, expected_child, proc_root):
        return result(
            label, "deaf", "poller-foreign", poller_pid=pid,
            _fingerprint=f"{owner_fingerprint}/{process_fingerprint(pid, proc_root)}",
        )
    return result(
        label, "healthy", "poller-owned", poller_pid=pid,
        _fingerprint=f"{owner_fingerprint}/{process_fingerprint(pid, proc_root)}",
    )


def revive_command(nurse_joy: pathlib.Path, label: str, session_id: str) -> list[str]:
    return [str(nurse_joy), "revive", label, "--resume", session_id, "--no-visit"]


def retry_disposition(
    entry: dict, *, current_time: float, cooldown: float, alert_cooldown: float, max_failures: int
) -> str:
    last_attempt = entry.get("last_attempt")
    failures = entry.get("failures", 0)
    if not isinstance(last_attempt, (int, float)):
        return "attempt"
    elapsed = current_time - float(last_attempt)
    if isinstance(failures, int) and failures >= max_failures and elapsed < alert_cooldown:
        return "alert-cooldown"
    if elapsed < cooldown:
        return "cooldown"
    return "attempt"


def confirmation_disposition(
    entry: dict, *, reason: str, fingerprint: str, current_time: float, confirm_seconds: float
) -> str:
    since = entry.get("deaf_since")
    if (
        entry.get("last_deaf_reason") != reason
        or entry.get("last_deaf_fingerprint") != fingerprint
        or not isinstance(since, (int, float))
    ):
        entry.update(
            {"deaf_since": current_time, "last_deaf_reason": reason, "last_deaf_fingerprint": fingerprint}
        )
        return "confirmed" if confirm_seconds <= 0 else "observe"
    return "confirmed" if current_time - float(since) >= confirm_seconds else "observe"


def default_nurse_joy() -> pathlib.Path:
    configured = os.environ.get("CLAUDE_POLLER_WATCHDOG_NURSE_JOY")
    if configured:
        return pathlib.Path(configured).expanduser()
    discovered = shutil.which("nurse-joy")
    if discovered:
        return pathlib.Path(discovered)
    local = pathlib.Path.home() / ".local/bin/nurse-joy"
    if local.exists():
        return local
    return pathlib.Path.home() / "HelmCortex/FORGE/bin/nurse-joy"


def discover_records(state_root: pathlib.Path) -> list[dict]:
    records: list[dict] = []
    for path in sorted(state_root.glob("last-session.*.json")):
        payload = read_json(path)
        if payload is not None:
            records.append(payload)
    return records


def emit(payload: dict, json_output: bool) -> None:
    payload = {key: value for key, value in payload.items() if not key.startswith("_")}
    if json_output:
        print(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")))
    else:
        print(f"{payload.get('label')}: {payload.get('status')} ({payload.get('reason')})")


def run(arguments: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    if arguments.expected_host and socket.gethostname() != arguments.expected_host:
        emit(result("fleet", "skip", "wrong-host", expected_host=arguments.expected_host), arguments.json)
        return 0
    state_root = pathlib.Path(arguments.state_root).expanduser()
    runtime_root = pathlib.Path(arguments.runtime_root).expanduser()
    channels_root = pathlib.Path(arguments.channels_root).expanduser()
    proc_root = pathlib.Path(arguments.proc_root)
    watchdog_root = pathlib.Path(arguments.watchdog_root).expanduser()
    watchdog_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    lock_path = watchdog_root / "watchdog.lock"
    state_path = watchdog_root / "state.json"
    with lock_path.open("a+", encoding="utf-8") as lock:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError:
            emit(result("fleet", "skip", "watchdog-already-running"), arguments.json)
            return 0
        state = read_json(state_path) or {"schema": SCHEMA, "targets": {}}
        targets = state.setdefault("targets", {})
        current_time = time.time()
        for record in discover_records(state_root):
            assessment = assess_record(
                record,
                state_root=state_root,
                runtime_root=runtime_root,
                channels_root=channels_root,
                proc_root=proc_root,
                current_time=current_time,
                startup_grace=arguments.startup_grace,
            )
            label = assessment["label"]
            if label == "unknown":
                emit(assessment, arguments.json)
                continue
            entry = targets.setdefault(label, {"failures": 0})
            if assessment["status"] == "healthy":
                entry.update({"failures": 0, "last_healthy": current_time, "last_reason": assessment["reason"]})
                entry.pop("deaf_since", None)
                entry.pop("last_deaf_reason", None)
                entry.pop("last_deaf_fingerprint", None)
                emit(assessment, arguments.json)
                continue
            if assessment["status"] != "deaf":
                emit(assessment, arguments.json)
                continue
            if confirmation_disposition(
                entry,
                reason=assessment["reason"],
                fingerprint=assessment["_fingerprint"],
                current_time=current_time,
                confirm_seconds=arguments.confirm_seconds,
            ) != "confirmed":
                assessment.update({"status": "suspect", "retry": "confirmation-pending"})
                emit(assessment, arguments.json)
                continue
            rechecked = assess_record(
                record,
                state_root=state_root,
                runtime_root=runtime_root,
                channels_root=channels_root,
                proc_root=proc_root,
                current_time=time.time(),
                startup_grace=arguments.startup_grace,
            )
            if (
                rechecked.get("status") != "deaf"
                or rechecked.get("reason") != assessment.get("reason")
                or rechecked.get("_fingerprint") != assessment.get("_fingerprint")
            ):
                emit(result(label, "skip", "pre-revive-revalidation-changed"), arguments.json)
                continue
            disposition = retry_disposition(
                entry,
                current_time=current_time,
                cooldown=arguments.cooldown,
                alert_cooldown=arguments.alert_cooldown,
                max_failures=arguments.max_failures,
            )
            if disposition != "attempt":
                assessment["status"] = "alert" if disposition == "alert-cooldown" else "skip"
                assessment["retry"] = disposition
                assessment["failures"] = entry.get("failures", 0)
                emit(assessment, arguments.json)
                continue
            command = revive_command(pathlib.Path(arguments.nurse_joy), label, record["session_id"])
            assessment["command"] = command
            if arguments.dry_run:
                assessment["status"] = "dry-run"
                emit(assessment, arguments.json)
                continue
            entry.update({"last_attempt": current_time, "last_reason": assessment["reason"]})
            # Persist the cooldown before invoking Nurse Joy.  If this process
            # is interrupted during a slow graceful exit, the next cron tick
            # must not launch an overlapping revive.
            state["updated_at"] = current_time
            atomic_json(state_path, state)
            try:
                completed = subprocess.run(
                    command,
                    stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                    stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                    stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                    timeout=arguments.revive_timeout,
                )
                returncode = completed.returncode
            except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
                returncode = 124
            if returncode == 0:
                entry.update({"failures": 0, "last_success": current_time})
                entry.pop("deaf_since", None)
                entry.pop("last_deaf_reason", None)
                entry.pop("last_deaf_fingerprint", None)
                assessment.update({"status": "revived", "returncode": 0})
            else:
                entry["failures"] = int(entry.get("failures", 0)) + 1
                assessment.update(
                    {"status": "alert" if entry["failures"] >= arguments.max_failures else "failed",
                     "returncode": returncode, "failures": entry["failures"]}
                )
            emit(assessment, arguments.json)
        state["updated_at"] = current_time
        if not arguments.dry_run:
            atomic_json(state_path, state)
    return 0


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    runtime = os.environ.get("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR", f"/run/user/{os.getuid()}")
    state = os.environ.get("XDG_STATE_HOME", str(pathlib.Path.home() / ".local/state"))
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--expected-host")
    parser.add_argument("--state-root", default=str(pathlib.Path(state) / "claude-idle-compaction"))
    parser.add_argument("--runtime-root", default=str(pathlib.Path(runtime) / "claude-idle-compaction"))
    parser.add_argument("--channels-root", default=str(pathlib.Path.home() / ".claude/channels"))
    parser.add_argument("--proc-root", default="/proc")
    parser.add_argument("--watchdog-root", default=str(pathlib.Path(state) / "claude-poller-watchdog"))
    parser.add_argument("--nurse-joy", default=str(default_nurse_joy()))
    parser.add_argument("--startup-grace", type=float, default=DEFAULT_STARTUP_GRACE_SECONDS)
    parser.add_argument("--confirm-seconds", type=float, default=DEFAULT_CONFIRM_SECONDS)
    parser.add_argument("--cooldown", type=float, default=DEFAULT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS)
    parser.add_argument("--alert-cooldown", type=float, default=DEFAULT_ALERT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS)
    parser.add_argument("--max-failures", type=int, default=DEFAULT_MAX_FAILURES)
    parser.add_argument("--revive-timeout", type=float, default=180.0)
    return parser


def main() -> int:
    return run(build_parser().parse_args())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
#+END_SRC

** Cron deployment helper

The source remains host-agnostic and contains no account name.  The explicit
apply command resolves the invoking user, home, and uid and installs a
three-minute =/etc/cron.d= schedule.  Tangling does not deploy it.

#+BEGIN_SRC bash :tangle all/.local/bin/claude-poller-watchdog-cron-apply :shebang "#!/usr/bin/env bash" :comments no
set -euo pipefail

TAG="claude-poller-watchdog-cron-apply"
TARGET="/etc/cron.d/dotcortex-claude-poller-watchdog"
REMOVE=0
DRY=0
EXPECTED_HOST=""

while [ "$#" -gt 0 ]; do
  case "$1" in
    --remove) REMOVE=1 ;;
    --dry-run) DRY=1 ;;
    --host)
      [ "$#" -ge 2 ] || { echo "$TAG: --host requires a value" >&2; exit 2; }
      EXPECTED_HOST="$2"
      shift ;;
    -h|--help|help)
      echo "Usage: $TAG --host EXACT_HOST [--dry-run] [--remove]"
      exit 0 ;;
    *) echo "$TAG: unknown argument: $1" >&2; exit 2 ;;
  esac
  shift
done

run_priv() { if [ "$(id -u)" = 0 ]; then "$@"; else sudo -n "$@"; fi; }

if [ "$REMOVE" = 1 ]; then
  if [ "$DRY" = 1 ]; then
    echo "remove $TARGET"
  else
    run_priv rm -f "$TARGET"
    echo "$TAG: removed $TARGET"
  fi
  exit 0
fi

[ -n "$EXPECTED_HOST" ] || { echo "$TAG: --host EXACT_HOST is required" >&2; exit 2; }
CURRENT_HOST="$(hostname)"
[ "$CURRENT_HOST" = "$EXPECTED_HOST" ] || {
  echo "$TAG: refusing installation on $CURRENT_HOST; expected $EXPECTED_HOST" >&2
  exit 1
}

USER_NAME="$(id -un)"
USER_ID="$(id -u)"
TARGET_HOME="$HOME"
WATCHDOG="$TARGET_HOME/.local/bin/claude-poller-watchdog"
LOG_DIR="$TARGET_HOME/.local/state/claude-poller-watchdog"
if [ "$DRY" != 1 ] && [ ! -x "$WATCHDOG" ]; then
  echo "$TAG: missing $WATCHDOG; tangle and stow first" >&2
  exit 1
fi

TMP="$(mktemp)"
trap 'rm -f "$TMP"' EXIT
cat >"$TMP" <<EOF
# DotCortex Claude Telegram poller watchdog; managed by $TAG.
*/3 * * * * ${USER_NAME} HOME=${TARGET_HOME} XDG_RUNTIME_DIR=/run/user/${USER_ID} ${WATCHDOG} --expected-host ${EXPECTED_HOST} --json >>${LOG_DIR}/cron.log 2>&1
EOF

if [ "$DRY" = 1 ]; then
  cat "$TMP"
else
  mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR"
  run_priv install -o root -g root -m 0644 "$TMP" "$TARGET"
  echo "$TAG: installed $TARGET"
fi
#+END_SRC

* Local summary governor regression tests

#+BEGIN_SRC python :tangle all/.local/share/dotcortex/tests/test-claude-warm-decider.py :comments no
import importlib.util
from importlib.machinery import SourceFileLoader
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import time
import unittest

def load(path, name):
    loader = SourceFileLoader(name, str(path))
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_loader(name, loader)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


class FakeSupervisor:
    def __init__(self, module):
        self.module = module
        self.obj = module.Supervisor.__new__(module.Supervisor)
        self.obj.enabled = True
        self.obj.status = "idle"
        self.obj.compacting = False
        self.obj.session_id = "123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000"
        self.obj.channel = "claude-test"
        self.obj.dirty = True
        self.obj.usage_limit_until = time.time() + 600
        self.obj.last_normal_stop = time.time() - 100
        self.obj.last_lifecycle_activity = None
        self.obj.last_local_input = None
        self.obj.transcript_activity_at = None
        self.obj.transcript_gate_blocked = False
        self.obj.transcript_gate_blocked_at = None
        self.obj.transcript_quiescence_deadline = None
        self.obj.recovery_pending = False
        self.obj.current_tokens = 100_000
        self.obj.post_compact_baseline_tokens = 0
        self.obj.cache_ttl_seconds = 3600
        self.obj.watched_servers = ()
        self.obj.recovery_command = None
